We find that Diff-SAE consistently and substantially outperforms Crosscoders for backdoor isolation. Diff-SAE achieves a Backdoor Isolation Score (BIS) of 0.40 with perfect precision (1.0) and zero false positive rate across most experimental conditions, while Crosscoders fail almost entirely with BIS below 0.02 in most cases. This performance gap holds across multiple transformer layers (14, 18, 22, 26) and both fine-tuning regimes, with full-rank fine-tuning producing particularly clean backdoor signals. Our results suggest that backdoors manifest as directional activation shifts rather than sparse feature activations, making difference-based representations fundamentally more effective for detection. These findings have important implications for AI safety monitoring and the development of interpretability tools for detecting model manipulation.

</details>


### [37] [Mean-Pooled Cosine Similarity is Not Length-Invariant: Theory and Cross-Domain Evidence for a Length-Invariant Alternative](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07345)
*Sibayan Mitra,Dhruv Kumar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文证明平均池化余弦相似度缺乏长度不变性：在Transformer各向异性表示下，该指标会随序列长度单调增长。实验显示长度比可解释跨语言表征相似性中R²=0.52-0.75的方差，而CKA等长度不变指标应作为默认比较度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前领域普遍使用平均池化余弦相似度作为跨语言、跨模态、跨任务神经表征比较的默认指标，但该指标是否真正公平地反映了表征间的相似性尚未被严格检验。

Method: 理论推导证明平均池化余弦相似度在Transformer各向异性表示下具有长度依赖性；在HumanEvalPack（4种代码LLM）、Mistral-7B（WMT平行语料）和CLIP ViT-B/32上进行实证验证，比较余弦相似度与CKA的行为差异。

Result: 长度比单独解释了跨语言"Python邻近性"中R²=0.52-0.75的方差，AST深度和共享token比例仅增加不到3%的解释方差；CKA将解释方差降低83%并反转长度系数符号。该模式在Mistral-7B的WMT数据中复现（余弦R²=0.23-0.33，CKA R²<0.01）。在CLIP中，平均池化相对EOS池化减少了长度效应（R²从0.21降至<0.01），符合理论预测。

Conclusion: CKA等长度不变指标应取代平均池化余弦相似度成为跨表征比较的默认度量；基于平均池化余弦相似度得出的跨语言表征趋同结论需要重新审视。

Abstract: Mean-pooled cosine similarity is the default metric for comparing neural representations across languages, modalities, and tasks. We establish that this metric is not length-invariant: under the anisotropy that characterizes modern transformer representations, mean-pooled cosine grows monotonically in sequence length, independent of representational content. Empirically, on HumanEvalPack across four code LLMs, the length ratio alone explains $R^2 = 0.52$--$0.75$ of cross-language "Python proximity," while AST depth and shared-token fraction add less than 3% of explained variance beyond length. Substituting Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA) reduces explained variance by 83% and reverses the sign of the length coefficient ($β_{\mathrm{len}}: +0.86 \to -0.37$). The same pattern holds in Mistral-7B on parallel WMT pairs ($R^2 = 0.23$ EN-FR, $R^2 = 0.33$ EN-DE for cosine; $R^2 < 0.01$ for CKA). In CLIP ViT-B/32, mean-pooling reduces the length effect relative to EOS-pooling ($R^2: 0.21 \to {<}0.01$), as predicted by the theory's dependence on anisotropy. We argue that length-invariant metrics such as CKA should be the default for cross-representation comparisons, and that recent claims of cross-lingual representational convergence built on mean-pooled cosine warrant re-examination.

</details>


### [38] [SSP-based construction of evaluation-annotated data for fine-grained aspect-based sentiment analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07446)
*Suwon Choi,Shinwoo Kim,Changhoe Hwang,Gwanghoon Yoo,Eric Laporte,Jeesun Nam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了韩语评价标注语料库EVAD，采用半自动符号传播（SSP）进行标注，并利用有限状态换能器（FST）构建语言资源，扩展了基于方面的情感分析（ABSA）框架，引入方面值（aspect values）分类（一元/二元/多元），在电商评论领域取得了KoBERT（F1=0.88）和KcBERT（F1=0.90）的稳健性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ABSA方法未能充分处理电商评论中情感与非情感语言模式的混合问题，且对目标的细粒度特征提取不够精确，缺乏对方面值（aspect values）的系统性分类与标注。

Method: 构建韩语EVAD语料库；采用半自动符号传播（SSP）进行标注；利用有限状态换能器（FST）构建语言资源；扩展ABSA框架，引入方面值分类（一元、二元、多元）；使用KoBERT和KcBERT进行模型训练与评估。

Result: KoBERT在方面-值对识别任务上达到F1=0.88，KcBERT达到F1=0.90，验证了所提标注方法和扩展ABSA框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 所构建的EVAD语料库和扩展ABSA方法能够更准确地分析用户意见并提取更详细的目标特征，基于BERT的模型在该任务上表现出色。

Abstract: We report the construction of a Korean evaluation-annotated corpus, hereafter called 'Evaluation Annotated Dataset (EVAD)', and its use in Aspect-Based Sentiment Analysis (ABSA) extended in order to cover e-commerce reviews containing sentiment and non-sentiment linguistic patterns. The annotation process uses Semi-Automatic Symbolic Propagation (SSP). We built extensive linguistic resources formalized as a Finite-State Transducer (FST) to annotate corpora with detailed ABSA components in the fashion e-commerce domain. The ABSA approach is extended, in order to analyze user opinions more accurately and extract more detailed features of targets, by including aspect values in addition to topics and aspects, and by classifying aspectvalue pairs depending whether values are unary, binary, or multiple. For evaluation, the KoBERT and KcBERT models are trained on the annotated dataset, showing robust performances of F1 0.88 and F1 0.90, respectively, on recognition of aspect-value pairs.

</details>


### [39] [Data Contamination in Neural Hieroglyphic Translation: A Reproducibility Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07453)
*Ammar Toutou,Abdelrahman Harb,Christine Basta*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现古埃及象形文字-德语神经机器翻译研究中存在严重的数据污染问题：原报告61.5 BLEU，实际复现仅37.0 BLEU，原因是2%的测试目标与训练数据重复（50%在8-gram重叠阈值下重合）。污染样本BLEU高达83.8，清洁样本仅30.9-39.2。文档级去重效果有限，需目标级去重。作者发布了34样本的清洁测试集和校正基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 古代和濒危语言的数据集天然稀缺、难以扩展且来源于公式化语料库，数据质量问题尤为严重但极少被审查。需要了解当前神经机器翻译对此类语言的实际能力。

Method: 复现先前M2M-100微调模型的象形文字-德语翻译结果，通过逐样本分析测试集与训练集的重叠情况，发现数据污染。对比五种模型配置（涵盖两种架构）在污染样本和清洁样本上的BLEU和COMET-22分数差异。分别进行文档级去重和目标级去重分析。

Result: 复现仅得37.0 BLEU（原报告61.5）。污染样本达83.8 BLEU/0.924 COMET-22，清洁样本仅30.9-39.2 BLEU/0.622-0.676 COMET-22。文档级去重仅降低4.6 BLEU，因8/16个污染目标通过其他源文档残留，需目标级去重。

Conclusion: 该濒危书写系统的NMT能力被严重高估，实际BLEU应为30.9-39.2。研究强调了对稀缺语言NMT评估中数据去污的必要性，并发布了去污测试集和校正基线。

Abstract: Ancient and endangered languages pose a unique challenge for NLP: their datasets are inherently scarce, difficult to expand, and built from formulaic corpora -- making data-quality issues especially consequential yet rarely audited. Motivated by the need to understand what current NMT can realistically achieve for such languages, we investigate hieroglyphic-to-German translation, where a recent study reported 61.5 BLEU using fine-tuned M2M-100. Our reproduction yields only 37.0 BLEU with the released model. Investigating this gap, we find 2\% of test targets appear identically in training (16/50; 50\% under 8-gram overlap at 70\% threshold). This contamination inflates scores dramatically: contaminated samples achieve up to 83.8 BLEU / 0.924 COMET-22 versus 30.9--39.2 BLEU / 0.622--0.676 COMET-22 on clean samples across five model configurations spanning two architectures. Document-level decontamination reduces contaminated BLEU by only 4.6 points because 8/16 targets persist via other source documents -- target-level deduplication is required. We release a decontaminated 34-sample test set and establish corrected baselines (30.9--39.2 BLEU), providing a realistic assessment of NMT capability for this endangered writing system.

</details>


### [40] [GRaSp: Automatic Example Optimization for In-Context Learning in Low-Data Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07454)
*Simen Bihaug-Frøyland,Henrik Brådland*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出GRaSp框架，通过"生成合成候选池→聚类降维结构化→遗传算法优化"三阶段自动选择上下文示例。在金融NER任务上，非合成数据下微F1达45.84%，优于零样本和随机少样本基线；合成数据仅匹配随机基线，表明候选池的分布多样性对泛化至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型上下文学习性能对所选示例高度敏感，但在领域特定、低数据场景中高质量示例稀缺，现有方法难以自动找到最优上下文示例。

Method: 三阶段框架GRaSp：(1) 生成大规模合成候选池；(2) 通过聚类和降维结构化候选池；(3) 使用遗传算法（含自定义多样性自适应变异机制）搜索最优示例组合，从初始跨簇探索过渡到后期簇内精炼。

Result: 在金融NER数据集FiNER-139上，非合成数据下GRaSp达45.84% micro-F1，优于零样本和随机少样本；合成数据仅匹配随机基线未超越。

Conclusion: GRaSp在非合成数据上持续有效；合成数据未超越随机基线，说明候选池的分布多样性比数量更重要，这是提升泛化能力的关键因素。

Abstract: In-context learning enables large language models to adapt to new tasks, but their performance is highly sensitive to the selected examples. Finding effective demonstrations is particularly difficult in domain-specific, low-data settings where high-quality examples are scarce. We propose GRaSp, a three-stage framework for automatic in-context example optimization. By first generating a large synthetic candidate pool, then structuring it with clustering and dimensionality reduction, and finally using genetic algorithms to find the optimal in-context examples, the framework shows consistent improvements on the NER task. We also introduce a custom diversity-adaptive mutation mechanism, allowing it to transition from the initial broad inter-cluster exploration to focused intra-cluster refinement as the population converges. We evaluate GRaSp on financial named entity recognition (FiNER-139), comparing synthetic and human-annotated candidate pools across pool sizes of 500 and 5000. With non-synthetic data, GRaSp achieves 45.84% micro-F1, consistently outperforming both zero-shot and random few-shot baselines. Synthetic data matches the random baseline but does not exceed it, suggesting that distributional variety in the candidate pool is critical for generalization.

</details>


### [41] [Think-with-Rubrics: From External Evaluator to Internal Reasoning Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07461)
*Jiachen Yu,Zhihao Xu,Junjie Wang,Yujiu Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Think-with-Rubrics范式，将评估准则（rubric）融入LLM的推理上下文，从外部评估器转变为内部生成引导，通过联合训练rubric生成与验证，在指令跟随任务中平均提升3.87个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于rubric的强化学习方法将rubric视为与策略推理轨迹脱钩的外部评估器，只能用于事后测量，无法主动引导模型的生成过程。

Method: 训练时LLM依次生成rubric和回答，同时训练rubric验证器对回答与自生成/黄金rubric之间的一致性进行联合监督，将rubric融入推理上下文作为内部引导。

Result: 在多个基准上，Think-with-Rubrics相比基于黄金rubric的Rubric-as-Reward基线平均提升3.87分。黄金rubric监督提升自生成rubric质量，自生成rubric监督提升回答内部一致性。

Conclusion: 将rubric从外部评估器转变为内部生成引导能够更有效地提升指令跟随任务的性能，两种监督信号从不同机制共同增强模型表现。

Abstract: Rubrics have been extensively utilized for evaluating unverifiable, open-ended tasks, with recent research incorporating them into reward systems for reinforcement learning. However, existing frameworks typically treat rubrics only as external evaluator disjointed from the policy's primary reasoning trace. Such design confines rubrics to post-hoc measurement, leaving them unable to actively guide the model's generation process. In this work, we introduce Think-with-Rubrics, a novel paradigm for instruction following tasks. Think-with-Rubrics integrates rubric generation into the reasoning context, transforming the rubric from an independent artifact into an internal guidance of LLM's generation. During training, LLM sequentially generates a rubric followed by a response, while a trained rubric verifier provides joint supervision by evaluating the consistency between the answer and the self-generated / golden rubrics. Experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate that Think-with-Rubrics consistently outperforms the Rubric-as-Reward baseline supervised by golden rubrics by an average of 3.87 points. We have also discussed the mechanism by which Think-with-Rubrics enhances model performance. Experimental results demonstrate that supervision from golden rubrics and self-generated rubrics enhances the performance of Think-with-Rubrics by improving the quality of self-generated rubrics and increasing the internal consistency of responses respectively.

</details>


### [42] [SEIF: Self-Evolving Reinforcement Learning for Instruction Following](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07465)
*Qingyu Ren,Qianyu He,Jiajie Zhu,Xingzhou Chen,Jingwen Chang,Zeye Sun,Han Xia,Fei Yu,Jiaqing Liang,Yanghua Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SEIF是一个自进化强化学习框架，通过指令难度进化与模型能力进化相互促进的闭环，持续提升大语言模型的指令跟随能力。该框架包含四个角色（生成指令的Instructor、过滤无效指令的Filter、学习跟随指令的Follower、提供奖励信号的Judger），通过交替训练实现共同进化，实验证明其有效性和通用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么依赖昂贵的外部监督（人类或强教师模型），要么使用静态难度的指令进行自对弈训练，指令难度无法随模型能力提升而进化，限制了指令跟随能力的持续提升。

Method: SEIF构建了一个闭环自进化框架，包含四个模块：Instructor生成渐进难度的指令，Filter过滤冲突或无效指令保证数据质量，Follower学习跟随进化后的指令，Judger提供强化学习的奖励信号。Instructor和Follower交替训练并共同进化。

Result: 在多种模型规模和架构上的实验表明，SEIF一致性地提升了指令跟随性能，具有良好的通用性。进一步分析发现有效的自进化训练策略是：前期充分训练建立坚实基础，后期适度训练避免过拟合以获得更好的最终性能。

Conclusion: SEIF通过自进化的强化学习框架有效解决了指令跟随能力的持续提升问题，无需外部监督；早期充分训练+后期适度训练是开放式任务自进化的有效策略。

Abstract: Instruction following is a fundamental capability of large language models (LLMs), yet continuously improving this capability remains challenging. Existing methods typically rely either on costly external supervision from humans or strong teacher models, or on self-play training with static-difficulty instructions that cannot evolve as the model's capabilities improve. To address these limitations, we propose SEIF (Self-Evolving Reinforcement Learning for Instruction Following), a self-evolving framework for enhancing the instruction-following ability of LLMs. SEIF forms a closed self-evolution loop that improves the model's instruction-following ability, where instruction difficulty evolution and model capability evolution reinforce each other. SEIF consists of four roles: an Instructor that generates increasingly challenging instructions, a Filter that removes conflicting or invalid instructions to ensure data quality, a Follower that learns to follow evolved instructions, and a Judger that provides reward signals for reinforcement learning. The Instructor and Follower are alternately trained and co-evolve throughout the process. Experiments across multiple model scales and architectures show that SEIF consistently improves instruction-following performance, suggesting strong generality. Further analyses reveal the sources of improvement and identify an effective training strategy for self-evolution on open-ended tasks: sufficient early-stage training to build a solid foundation, followed by moderate late-stage training to mitigate overfitting and achieve better final performance. The code and data are publicly available at https://github.com/Rainier-rq1/SEIF.

</details>


### [43] [TCMIIES: A Browser-Based LLM-Powered Intelligent Information Extraction System for Academic Literature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07507)
*Hanqing Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TCMIIES，一个基于浏览器的零安装平台，利用商业LLM API对学术文献进行结构化信息提取。系统采用模式引导的提示框框架和自动系统提示生成，支持用户通过图形界面自定义提取模式而无需编程。纯前端架构确保数据隐私，支持五大LLM提供商，实现并发批处理和自动重试机制，并为中文学术数据库提供智能字段映射。在中医药研究中，结构化输出合规率超过94%，信息提取准确率与领域专家标注相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学术出版物呈指数级增长，亟需自动化工具从非结构化科学文本中提取结构化知识。现有解决方案需要专门基础设施、编程专业知识或微调领域特定模型，给专业领域研究人员设置了障碍。

Method: TCMIIES系统采用纯前端浏览器架构，通过模式引导的提示框框架自动生成系统提示，用户可通过图形界面自定义提取模式；支持五家主要LLM提供商的API，实现并发批处理与自动重试机制，并为CNKI和万方等中文数据库提供智能字段映射。

Result: 在中医药研究多个提取场景中，系统结构化输出合规率超过94%，信息提取准确率达到与领域专家标注相当的水平。

Conclusion: TCMIIES是一个实用、易获取的解决方案，弥合了先进LLM能力与领域特定学术信息提取需求之间的差距，特别适合需要灵活、隐私保护且成本效益高的提取工具的专业领域研究人员。

Abstract: The exponential growth of academic publications has created an urgent need for automated tools capable of extracting structured knowledge from unstructured scientific texts. While large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in natural language understanding and information extraction, existing solutions often require specialized infrastructure, programming expertise, or fine-tuned domain-specific models that create barriers for researchers in specialized fields. This paper presents TCMIIES, a browser-based, zero-installation platform that leverages commercial LLM APIs to perform structured information extraction from academic literature. The system employs a novel schema-guided prompting framework with automatic system prompt generation, enabling researchers to define custom extraction schemas through an intuitive graphical interface without any programming. TCMIIES features a pure front-end architecture that ensures data privacy by processing all information locally in the browser, supports five major LLM providers, implements concurrent batch processing with automatic retry mechanisms, and provides intelligent field mapping for Chinese academic databases including CNKI and Wanfang. We demonstrate the system's effectiveness through comprehensive evaluation across multiple extraction scenarios in Traditional Chinese Medicine research, achieving structured output compliance rates exceeding 94\% and information extraction accuracy comparable to domain-expert annotation. The system represents a practical, accessible solution that bridges the gap between advanced LLM capabilities and domain-specific academic information extraction needs, particularly for researchers in specialized fields who require flexible, privacy-preserving, and cost-effective extraction tools.

</details>


### [44] [WeatherSyn: An Instruction Tuning MLLM For Weather Forecasting Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07522)
*Zinan Zheng,Yang Liu,Nuo Chen,Juepeng Zheng,Hong Cheng,Jia Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了天气预报报告(WFR)任务，构建了首个指令微调数据集，并开发了专门生成天气预报报告的MLLM模型\DatasetNameL，该模型在多项指标上优于闭源MLLM，并展现出强大的跨区域零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前天气预报报告主要依赖人工分析多源数据，导致信息过载和效率低下，而多模态大语言模型在该领域的应用尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出了WFR任务，构建了覆盖美国31个城市、8个天气维度的指令微调数据集\DatasetNameL，并基于该语料开发了专门用于生成天气预报报告的模型\ModelNameL。

Result: \ModelNameL在多项评估指标上持续优于主流闭源MLLM，尤其在结构复杂的天气方面表现突出；模型在不同地理区域和天气维度上均展现出强大的迁移能力和零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: \ModelNameL为开发专门用于天气预报报告生成的MLLM提供了有价值的见解。

Abstract: Accurate weather forecast reporting enables individuals and communities to better plan daily activities and agricultural operations. However, the current reporting process primarily relies on manual analysis of multi-source data, which leads to information overload and reduced efficiency. With the development of multimodal large language models (MLLMs), leveraging data-driven models to analyze and generate reports in the weather forecasting domain remains largely underexplored. In this work, we propose the Weather Forecasting Report (WFR) task and construct the first instruction-tuning dataset for this task, named~\DatasetNameL, which covers 31 cities in America and 8 weather aspects. Based on this corpus, we develop the first model, \ModelNameL, specialized in generating weather forecast reports. Evaluation across multiple metrics on our dataset shows that \ModelNameL~ consistently outperforms leading closed-source MLLMs, particularly on structurally complex weather aspects. We further analyze its performance across diverse geographic regions and weather aspects. \ModelNameL~ demonstrates strong transferability across different regions, highlighting its zero-shot generalization capability. \ModelNameL~offers valuable insight for developing MLLMs specialized in weather report generation. .

</details>


### [45] [Why do Large Language Models Fail in Low-resource Translation? Unraveling the Token Dynamics of Large Language Models for Machine Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07533)
*Shenbin Qian,Yves Scherrer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过评估15个LLM（含4个推理型LLM）在22个语言对上的翻译表现，系统分析了LLM在机器翻译中的失败模式。研究发现非英语中心语言对的COMET分数更低，为此引入Token激活率（TAR）指标衡量模型对词汇表中特定语言token的利用效率，证明TAR与翻译质量强相关，并发现推理型LLM在低TAR语言上会生成更多token作为补偿机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注提升或评估LLM的翻译质量，但对LLM翻译何时失败以及为何失败的理解有限，缺乏系统性的失败模式分析。

Method: 评估15个LLM（含4个推理型LLM）在22个语言对上的翻译表现，引入Token激活率（TAR）指标量化模型对特定语言token的利用效率，并分析TAR与翻译质量之间的关系，以及推理型LLM在低TAR语言上的补偿行为。

Result: 非英语中心语言对的COMET分数显著低于英语中心语言对；TAR与翻译质量呈强正相关；推理型LLM在低TAR语言上倾向于生成更多token，但该补偿对翻译质量的影响因模型而异。

Conclusion: token级动态（如TAR）是理解LLM翻译性能的关键因素，强调了词汇表中语言特定token的利用率对翻译质量的重要影响。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently demonstrated strong performance in machine translation (MT). However, most prior work focuses on improving or benchmarking translation quality, offering limited insight into when and why LLM-based translation fails. In this work, we systematically analyze failure modes of LLMs in MT by evaluating 15 models, including four reasoning LLMs, across 22 language pairs (LPs) with varying resource levels. We find that non-English-centric LPs consistently yield lower COMET scores than English-centric pairs. To investigate the underlying causes, we introduce Token Activation Rate (TAR), a metric that captures how effectively a model utilizes language-specific tokens in its vocabulary during generation. We validate TAR as a proxy for language representation using models with known language distributions in the training data, and show that lower TAR is strongly associated with poorer translation performance. Furthermore, reasoning LLMs tend to generate more tokens when translating into low-TAR languages, suggesting a compensatory mechanism, although its impact on translation quality varies across models. Overall, our findings emphasize the importance of token-level dynamics in understanding MT performance of LLMs.

</details>


### [46] [Nürnberg NLP at PsyDefDetect: Multi-Axis Voter Ensembles for Psychological Defence Mechanism Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07606)
*Philipp Steigerwald,Eric Rudolph,Jens Albrecht*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对心理防御机制检测中类别边界模糊的问题，提出一个9模型集成系统，沿类别粒度、训练方法和基础模型三个正交轴构建，在PsyDefDetect任务中取得F1=0.420，在21个参赛队伍中排名第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心理防御机制检测任务中，八个正面防御类别在表层语言上相似，仅语用功能不同，标注者间一致性仅为中等水平。单一模型在重叠的防御边界上表现不稳定，因此需要利用错误独立性的集成策略来提升性能。

Method: 构建一个9投票器集成系统，沿三个正交轴设计：(1) 类别粒度——门控模型使用全部9类，专家模型仅使用8个防御类别；(2) 训练方法——同时使用生成式与判别式方法；(3) 基础模型——使用不同的预训练模型。通过错误独立性在重叠类别边界上获得鲁棒性。

Result: 在隐藏测试集上达到F1=0.420，在21个注册队伍中排名第一。

Conclusion: 对于类别边界模糊、标注者一致性不高的细粒度分类任务，利用多轴多样性构建集成系统、追求错误独立性，比追求单一强模型更为有效。

Abstract: Detecting levels of psychological defence mechanisms in supportive conversations is inherently ambiguous. In the PsyDefDetect shared task at BioNLP 2026 the eight positive defence categories share surface language and differ only in pragmatic function and trained raters reach only moderate inter-annotator agreement. On such a task the decisive lever is not a stronger single model but error independence, since any single representation will waver on the overlapping defence boundaries. We translate this insight into a 9-voter ensemble spanning three orthogonal axes: class granularity (all nine classes for the gatekeeper, only the eight defence classes for the specialists), training method (generative and discriminative) and base model. The system reaches $F1_{test}{=}.420$ on the hidden test set, placing first among 21 registered teams.

</details>


### [47] [Intent-Driven Semantic ID Generation for Grounded Conversational News Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07613)
*Hongyang Su,Beibei Kong,Lei Cheng,Chengxiang Zhuo,Zang Li,Chenyun Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出面向对话新闻推荐的意图驱动语义ID生成方法，采用"生成-匹配"范式，通过两阶段训练（多任务SID对齐+GPT-4思维链蒸馏）将多样化意图映射为层次化语义ID前缀，再与新闻池进行模糊匹配。在主流新闻平台上，7B模型在152K开放生成SID空间中实现0%幻觉和12.4% L1匹配（4倍随机基线），以约100倍低成本匹配GPT-4+混合RAG的L1性能并在细粒度指标上超越，冷启动用户达到18.0% L1（6倍随机）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话新闻推荐需要在快速演化的文章语料库中进行推荐，同时处理缺乏显式可检索关键词的隐式用户意图。标准RAG流程因存在"检索优先"瓶颈而难以应对这些隐式意图类型。

Method: 提出意图驱动的语义ID生成方法，采用Generate-then-Match范式。两阶段训练：第一阶段进行多任务SID对齐，第二阶段通过GPT-4思维链蒸馏。利用Profile-Aware Dual-Signal Reasoning (PADR)实现冷启动用户推荐。

Result: 7B模型在152K开放生成SID空间中实现0%幻觉率和12.4% L1匹配（4倍随机基线）。以约100倍低成本匹配GPT-4+Hybrid RAG的L1性能，并在L2（2倍）和Category（+1.2pp）上超越。冷启动用户达到18.0% L1匹配（6倍随机），为所有用户组中最高。

Conclusion: 提出意图驱动的语义ID生成方法有效解决了对话新闻推荐中的隐式意图和检索瓶颈问题，在性能与成本之间取得了显著优势，尤其对冷启动场景具有突破性效果。

Abstract: Conversational news recommendation requires grounding each suggestion in a rapidly evolving article corpus while addressing implicit user intents that lack explicit retrievable keywords. To characterize this scenario, we identify 6 intent types from production dialogues: five are implicit and pose fundamental challenges to standard RAG pipelines, forming a critical retrieve-first bottleneck. To address these issues, we introduce intent-driven Semantic ID (SID) generation under a Generate-then-Match paradigm. With two-stage training that consists of multi-task SID alignment and GPT-4 Chain-of-Thought distillation, an LLM maps diverse intents to hierarchical SID prefixes, which are then fuzzy-matched to the current news pool to guarantee fully grounded recommendations. Profile-Aware Dual-Signal Reasoning (PADR) further enables cold-start users to obtain valid recommendations using only profiles. On a mainstream Chinese news platform, our 7B model achieves 0% hallucination and 12.4% L1 match in the 152K open-generation SID space (4x random baseline). It matches GPT-4+Hybrid RAG on L1 while surpassing it on finer-grained metrics (L2 2x, Category +1.2pp) at ~100x lower cost. Cold-start users, where existing baselines score 0%, achieve 18.0% L1 (6x random), the highest among all user groups.

</details>


### [48] [Is She Even Relevant? When BERT Ignores Explicit Gender Cues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07622)
*Jonas Klein,Chiara Manna,Eva Vanmassenhove*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过从头训练一个荷兰语BERT模型，研究了性别信息在训练过程中何时以及如何出现。研究发现：性别在第20个epoch左右变得线性可分，但模型无法很好地利用显式性别线索（如"Zij is een loodgieter"）来更新内部性别表示，导致反刻板印象配对（如女水管工）预测准确率低，且荷兰语中的通用形式默认偏向男性解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注英语中的性别偏见，而带有语法性别的语言（如荷兰语）研究不足。本文旨在探究在兼具显性形态性别标记和通用形式的荷兰语中，BERT模型何时以及如何形成性别表征，并检验上下文嵌入是否能根据显式线索动态调整其性别表示。

Method: 从头训练一个荷兰语BERT模型，在训练全程提取各检查点的上下文嵌入；使用线性SVM构建动态性别子空间，追踪性别编码的线性可分性和演化过程；设计受控句子模板（如"Zij is een loodgieter"）测试显式性别线索是否能覆盖统计关联（如水管工→男性）。

Result: 性别表示大约在第20个epoch变得线性可分，并分布在多个嵌入维度上；模型无法在短句子模板中根据显式上下文线索更新内部性别表示；反刻板印象的职业-性别配对准确率远低于刻板印象配对；荷兰语中的通用形式即使上下文明确指向女性，也默认采用男性解释。

Conclusion: 荷兰语BERT模型所学到的上下文表示在性别方向上的动态性不足，显式性别线索在反刻板印象上下文中未能可靠地反映在表示中，导致持续的男性默认行为。

Abstract: Gender bias in large language models has primarily been investigated for English, while languages with grammatical or morphological gender remain comparatively understudied. This paper investigates how and when gender information emerges in a Dutch BERT model trained from scratch, offering one of the first checkpoint-level analyses of bias formation in a Transformer architecture for a language combining overt morphological gender marking and generic forms. By extracting contextual embeddings throughout training, we construct dynamic gender subspaces using linear SVMs to trace when gender becomes linearly encoded and how this encoding evolves over time. Contextual embeddings are often assumed to integrate contextual cues robustly, allowing models to adjust the representation of a word depending on its more local usage. We therefore test whether explicit gender cues in controlled sentence templates (e.g., Zij is een loodgieter ('She is a plumber')) can override learned statistical associations (plumber -> male). Our findings challenge this assumption: although gender becomes clearly linearly separable around epoch 20 and is distributed across multiple embedding dimensions, the model struggles to update its internal gender representation in light of explicit contextual cues in short sentence templates. Stereotypical gender-profession pairings are predicted far more accurately than anti-stereotypical ones, and generic forms in Dutch systematically default to a male interpretation, even when the context explicitly denotes a female referent. Together, our results seem to indicate that contextualization in the representations learned by our Dutch BERT model is not sufficiently dynamic along the probed gender direction: explicit gender cues in anti-stereotypical contexts are not reliably reflected in the resulting representations, resulting in persistent male-default behaviour.

</details>


### [49] [Multi-Dimensional Evaluation of LLMs for Grammatical Error Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07635)
*Adnan Labib,Qiao Wang,Yixuan Huang,Zheng Yuan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估了最新大语言模型在语法纠错任务上的表现，发现微调后的GPT-4o达到最高性能；不同LLM纠错模式高度相似（ρ=0.947）；73.76%被参考标准判定为错误的GPT-4o纠错结果实际上同样正确或更优，表明基于参考的指标严重低估了GEC系统性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语法纠错领域存在三个关键缺口：最新LLM缺乏全面评估、LLM组合能否提升纠错质量未知、基于参考的指标对GEC系统性能的低估程度未被充分量化。

Method: 评估最新LLM在编辑精度、流利度保持和语义保留三个维度上的表现；通过语法错误类型分析对比不同LLM的纠错模式；量化参考指标对GEC性能的低估程度。

Result: 微调GPT-4o在三项指标上均达到最优；各LLM纠错模式高度一致（ρ=0.947）；73.76%被参考标注判定不同的GPT-4o修正实际同等有效或更优。

Conclusion: 基于参考的指标显著低估GEC系统性能，研究结果为教育者选择语法纠错助手提供了指导依据。

Abstract: Automated assistants for Grammatical Error Correction are now embedded in educational platforms serving millions of learners, yet three critical gaps remain in this domain: (1) latest-generation Large Language Models (LLMs) lack comprehensive evaluation on grammar correction tasks; (2) whether combining these LLMs improves correction quality is unexplored; and (3) the extent to which reference-based metrics underestimate GEC system performance has not been adequately quantified. In this study, first, we evaluate latest-generation LLMs on edit precision, fluency preservation, and meaning retention, showing fine-tuned GPT-4o achieves state-of-the-art performance across all three dimensions. Second, through grammatical error type analysis we demonstrate that individual LLMs exhibit highly similar error correction patterns ($ρ=0.947$). Third, we show that reference-based metrics underestimate GEC performance with 73.76% of GPT-4o corrections different from gold standards being equally valid or even superior. These GEC evaluation findings equip educators with guidance for selecting GEC assistants that enhance rather than constrain student linguistic development. We make our data, code, and models publicly available.

</details>


### [50] [MAVEN: Multi-Agent Verification-Elaboration Network with In-Step Epistemic Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07646)
*Yinsheng Yao,Jiehao Tang,Zhaozhen Yang,Dawei Cheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MAVEN是一种黑板式多智能体框架，通过显式的怀疑者-研究者-裁判对抗循环，将LLM转化为可审计的审慎推理系统，在多个基准上超越隐式推理和共识基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理范式依赖缺乏中间验证的链条式推理，早期错误会级联传播；缺乏模块化设计阻碍了细粒度审计，在高风险应用中损害了认知可信度。

Method: 提出MAVEN框架，采用黑板架构，设计对抗性的怀疑者-研究者-裁判循环：通过角色解耦（逻辑辩护与事实依据分离）模拟专家审辩过程，实现显式结构化、模块化且可验证的推理轨迹生成。

Result: 在OpenBookQA、TruthfulQA、HALUEVAL和StrategyQA基准上，MAVEN在四个细粒度指标上均展现优越推理质量，持续优于GEMINI-3.1-Pro和ReConcile等基线；同时完全模型无关，可显著提升多种骨干模型的性能。

Conclusion: MAVEN通过显式结构化的审慎推理轨迹替代隐式内部状态或事后共识，有效提升了推理质量与可审计性，是一种强且可迁移的通用推理增强方法。

Abstract: While explicit reasoning trajectories enhance model interpretability, existing paradigms often rely on monolithic chains that lack intermediate verification, allowing early errors to cascade unchecked. This lack of modularity impedes granular auditing and compromises the epistemic trust required for high-stakes applications. We propose MAVEN (Multi-Agent Verification-Elaboration Network with In-Step Epistemic Auditing), a blackboard-inspired framework designed to transform LLMs into deliberate reasoners through explicit role-decoupling. At its core, MAVEN operationalizes an adversarial Skeptic-Researcher-Judge loop, simulating expert deliberation by functionally separating logical defense from factual grounding. Experiments on OpenBookQA, TruthfulQA, HALUEVAL and StrategyQA benchmarks demonstrate that MAVEN delivers superior reasoning quality across four fine-grained metrics. Notably, MAVEN consistently outperforms latent reasoning models such as GEMINI-3.1-Pro and consensus-based baselines (e.g., ReConcile) by generating explicitly structured, modular, and verifiable deliberation trajectories, rather than relying on implicit internal states or post-hoc consensus. Moreover, comprehensive evaluations confirm that MAVEN is fully model-agnostic, serving as a strong and transferable reasoning booster that yields substantial performance improvements across diverse backbone models.

</details>


### [51] [Quality-Conditioned Agreement in Automated Short Answer Scoring: Mid-Range Degradation and the Impact of Task-Specific Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07647)
*Abigail Victoria Gurin Schleifer,Moriah Ariely,Beata Beigman Klebanov,Asaf Salman,Giora Alexandron*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究比较了不同AI模型（few-shot LLMs、微调BERT）与人类专家在自动简答题评分中的表现，发现所有AI模型在中等质量（部分正确）答案上存在显著的评分退化，且这种退化程度与任务特定适应度相关：few-shot LLMs最严重，微调模型较好，但均不如人类专家。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动简答题评分正从判别式微调模型转向大语言模型的few-shot范式，但这种范式在需要细微解读的部分正确回答上的表现尚不明确，可能影响评分公平性。

Method: 比较三种few-shot LLMs（GPT-5.2、GPT-4o、Claude Opus 4.5）、微调BERT编码器模型和人类专家在两个开放式生物学题目上的评分一致性，使用数百份学生回答及生物学教育专家提供的地面真值分数。

Result: 人类-人类一致性最高且在所有质量区间稳定；所有AI模型在完全正确和完全错误的回答上表现良好，但在中等质量回答上出现显著退化；这种退化程度与任务特定适应度相关：few-shot LLMs最严重，随任务特定数据增加而减弱，微调编码器模型表现最佳。

Conclusion: 中等质量回答的评分退化可能导致对理解发展中的学生产生不公平评价，研究强调了基于质量条件公平性的重要性，需特别关注中等质量回答。

Abstract: Automated short answer scoring (ASAS) is shifting from discriminative, fine-tuned models to large language models (LLMs) used in few-shot settings. This paradigm leverages LLMs broad world knowledge and ease of deployment, but limited task-specific data may reduce alignment on complex scoring tasks. In particular, its impact on scoring partially correct responses that require nuanced interpretation remains underexplored. We investigate the relationship between the degree of task-specific adaptation of different models and quality-conditioned scoring agreement. We compare three LLMs (GPT-5.2, GPT-4o, Claude Opus 4.5) in few-shot mode, a fine-tuned BERT-based encoder, and a human expert on two open-ended biology items, using several hundred student responses and ground truth scores provided by a biology education expert. The results show that human-human agreement is highest and stable across the full quality spectrum. All AI models perform well on fully correct and fully incorrect responses, but exhibit substantial degradation on mid-range responses. This mid-range degradation is conditioned on task-specific adaptation: It is most severe in few-shot LLMs with few examples and decreases as task-specific data increases, with fine-tuned encoder models performing best. This mid-range degradation may lead to inequitable evaluation of responses produced by students with developing understanding. Our findings highlight the importance of quality-conditioned fairness, with particular attention to mid-range responses.

</details>


### [52] [Not All Tokens Learn Alike: Attention Entropy Reveals Heterogeneous Signals in RL Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07660)
*Gengyang Li,Zheng-Fan Wu,Siqi Bao,Yunfang Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过注意力熵揭示强化学习后训练中token级信号的异质性，将token分为低熵的"锚点"（提供稳定优化骨干）和高熵的"探索者"（提供困难推理信号但梯度不稳定），并基于此提出动态熵感知软重加权方法，在Qwen3-8B-Base上提升3个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习后训练显著提升了大语言模型的推理能力，但token级学习信号的本质仍不清楚，缺乏对其异质性的系统性理解。

Method: 1）通过注意力熵度量每个响应token的上下文支持集中程度；2）设计稀疏估计实验和熵结构子集训练；3）提出锚点-探索者双谱系分析框架；4）引入动态熵感知软重加权干预方法。

Result: 1）均匀随机20%token子集即可保留大部分全token性能，表明token级更新存在大量冗余；2）低熵锚点提供稳定对齐的梯度，但高难度基准上易饱和；3）高熵探索者梯度波动大，平均训练不稳定，但少数运行表明其中包含困难推理信号；4）动态熵感知软重加权将Qwen3-8B-Base从34.39提升至37.40。

Conclusion: 注意力熵揭示了RL后训练中token级优化相关的结构，统一token平均会掩盖有意义的异质性；熵感知的加权策略能够有效利用这种异质性提升模型性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement-learning-based post-training has become a key approach for improving the reasoning ability of large language models, but its token-level learning signals remain poorly understood. This work studies their heterogeneity through attention entropy, which measures how concentrated or diffuse the contextual support is for each response token.
  We first show that token-level RL objectives are sparsely estimable: uniformly random 20 percent token subsets preserve much of the full-token held-out performance, suggesting substantial redundancy in token-level updates. However, entropy-structured subsets behave very differently. Low-attention-entropy tokens, which we call anchors, rely on concentrated support, produce stable gradients aligned with full-token updates, and provide a reliable optimization backbone, but tend to plateau on harder benchmarks. High-attention-entropy tokens, which we call explorers, aggregate more diffuse context and induce larger but more volatile gradients. Explorer-only training is unstable on average, though rare successful runs suggest that these tokens may contain useful hard-reasoning signals when optimization remains stable.
  We support this anchor-explorer spectrum with evidence-gathering analyses, entropy dynamics, gradient-geometry diagnostics, and controls showing that position, predictive entropy, and loss normalization do not explain the observed asymmetry. Finally, a dynamic entropy-aware soft-reweighting intervention improves Qwen3-8B-Base from 34.39 to 37.40 held-out average in the strongest setting. These findings suggest that attention entropy reveals optimization-relevant structure in token-level RL signals, and that uniform token averaging can obscure meaningful heterogeneity in reasoning post-training.

</details>


### [53] [DRIP-R: A Benchmark for Decision-Making and Reasoning Under Real-World Policy Ambiguity in the Retail Domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07699)
*Hsuvas Borkakoty,Sebastian Pohl,Cheng Wang,Bei Chen,Yufang Hou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DRIP-R是一个利用真实零售政策歧义构建的基准测试，用于评估LLM智能体在无单一正确解决方案的模糊场景中的决策能力。实验表明，前沿模型在面对相同政策歧义场景时存在根本性分歧，证实歧义对LLM决策构成系统性挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体基准测试大多假设策略是明确无歧义的，但现实世界中领域策略往往存在多种合理解释，导致关键评估空白。

Method: 收集真实零售政策歧义场景，构建包含策略模糊退货场景、逼真客户画像、全双工会话模拟（支持工具调用）以及多裁判评估框架（覆盖策略遵从度、对话质量、行为对齐和解决方案质量）的基准测试。

Result: 前沿模型在面对相同的策略歧义场景时存在根本性分歧。

Conclusion: 政策歧义对LLM决策构成了真实且系统性的挑战，现有模型无法妥善处理此类模糊情境。

Abstract: LLM-based agents are increasingly deployed for routine but consequential tasks in real-world domains, where their behavior is governed by inherently ambiguous domain policies that admit multiple valid interpretations. Despite the prevalence of such ambiguities in practice, existing agent benchmarks largely assume unambiguous, well-specified policies, leaving a critical evaluation gap. We introduce DRIP-R, a benchmark that systematically exploits real-world retail policy ambiguities to construct scenarios in which no single correct resolution exists. DRIP-R comprises a curated set of policy-ambiguous return scenarios paired with a realistic customer personas, a full-duplex conversational simulation with tool-calling capabilities and a multi-judge evaluation framework covering policy adherence, dialogue quality, behavioral alignment, and resolution quality. Our experiments show that frontier models fundamentally disagree on identical policy-ambiguous scenarios, confirming that ambiguity poses a genuine and systematic challenge to LLM decision-making.

</details>


### [54] [Guidance Is Not a Hyperparameter: Learning Dynamic Control in Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07701)
*Fan Zhou,Tim Van de Cruys*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 将无分类器引导(CFG)的尺度选择建模为序列决策问题，通过强化学习（PPO）学习动态引导轨迹，在离散扩散语言模型的受控文本生成任务中相比固定尺度策略取得了更好的可控性与质量平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的CFG引导尺度在整个生成过程中被固定为静态超参数，导致可控性与生成质量之间的折衷是次优的，因为最优引导程度在不同任务和扩散过程的不同阶段是变化的，尤其在NLP领域更为突出。

Method: 将引导尺度建模为每个生成步骤基于演化扩散状态选择的离散控制动作，使用近端策略优化（PPO）在任务级奖励下优化策略，在离散扩散语言模型上进行三个受控NLP生成任务的实验。

Result: 自适应引导策略在可控性与生成质量之间始终优于固定尺度策略；学习到的策略在不同任务上展现出清晰可解释的不同引导轨迹。

Conclusion: 应将CFG引导视为一个动态控制过程而非静态设计选择，证明了基于强化学习的自适应引导方法的有效性。

Abstract: Classifier-Free Guidance (CFG) is a widely used mechanism for controlling diffusion-based generative models, yet its guidance scale is typically treated as a fixed hyperparameter throughout generation. This static design yields a suboptimal controllability and quality tradeoff, as the optimal degree of guidance varies across tasks and across different stages of the diffusion process, especially in NLP domain. We recast CFG scale selection as a sequential decision-making problem and propose to learn dynamic guidance trajectories via reinforcement learning. Specifically, we model the guidance scale as a discrete control action selected at each generation step based on the evolving diffusion state, and optimize a policy using Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) under task-level rewards. Experiments on three controlled NLP generation tasks using discrete diffusion language models demonstrate that adaptive guidance consistently achieves a better balance between controllability and generation quality than fixed-scale strategies. Further analysis of the learned policies reveals distinct and interpretable guidance trajectories across tasks, underscoring the importance of treating guidance as a dynamic control process rather than a static design choice.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [55] [GraphDC: A Divide-and-Conquer Multi-Agent System for Scalable Graph Algorithm Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06671)
*Wenjin Li,Jiaming Cui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了GraphDC，一个基于分治策略的多智能体框架，用于可扩展的图算法推理。它将大图分解为小子图，分配专门智能体进行局部推理，再由主智能体整合信息，显著提升了LLM在图算法任务上的表现，尤其在大图实例上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLMs在图算法推理任务上表现不佳，因为图在拓扑结构上天然复杂，需要系统性的多步推理，尤其是在处理更大规模图时，端到端推理方法的可靠性显著下降。

Method: 采用分治策略的多智能体框架GraphDC：（1）将输入图分解为较小的子图；（2）每个子图分配给专门的智能体进行局部推理；（3）主智能体整合局部输出与子图间信息，生成最终解。这种层级化设计降低了单个智能体的推理负担，缓解了计算瓶颈。

Result: 在多种图算法推理任务和不同规模上，GraphDC一致性地优于现有方法，尤其在更大规模的图实例上，直接端到端推理不可靠的情况下表现更为突出。

Conclusion: 分治策略的多智能体框架能够有效提升LLM在图算法推理任务上的可扩展性和鲁棒性，为解决复杂图推理问题提供了一种有效范式。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong potential for many mathematical problems. However, their performance on graph algorithmic tasks is still unsatisfying, since graphs are naturally more complex in topology and often require systematic multi-step reasoning, especially on larger graphs. Motivated by this gap, we propose GraphDC, a Divide-and-Conquer multi-agent framework for scalable graph algorithm reasoning. Specifically, inspired by Divide-and-Conquer design, GraphDC decomposes an input graph into smaller subgraphs, assigns each subgraph to a specialized agent for local reasoning, and uses a master agent to integrate the local outputs with inter-subgraph information to produce the final solution. This hierarchical design reduces the reasoning burden on individual agents, alleviates computational bottlenecks, and improves robustness on large graph instances. Extensive experiments show that GraphDC consistently outperforms existing methods on graph algorithm reasoning across diverse tasks and scales, especially on larger instances where direct end-to-end reasoning is less reliable.

</details>


### [56] [More Thinking, More Bias: Length-Driven Position Bias in Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06672)
*Xiao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现，在推理模型中，位置偏见与推理轨迹长度呈正相关（12/13配置），而非如通常假设的那样通过思考减少启发式偏差。截断实验提供因果证据，且链式推理会替代直接答案的位置偏见，转化为随长度累积的偏见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战"推理模型通过仔细思考减少浅层启发式偏差"的常见假设，检验推理轨迹长度与位置偏见之间的关系。

Method: 在13种推理配置（含R1蒸馏模型、基础模型CoT提示、671B DeepSeek-R1）上，对MMLU、ARC-Challenge、GPQA数据集计算位置偏见分数与轨迹长度的偏相关，并进行截断干预实验获取因果证据。

Result: 12/13配置中轨迹长度与位置偏见分数呈显著正偏相关（0.11-0.41）；截断后继续生成时转向位置偏好选项的比例增加16%-32%；671B模型聚合偏见近乎消失（PBS=0.019）但最长四分位仍显著（0.071）；直接回答与CoT推理的位置偏见是不同现象。

Conclusion: 推理模型不应被视为默认对选项顺序鲁棒，应使用诊断工具包（PBS、承诺变化点、有效切换、截断探针）审计位置偏见。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning and reasoning-tuned models such as DeepSeek-R1 are commonly assumed to reduce shallow heuristic biases by thinking carefully. We test this on position bias in multiple-choice QA and find a different story: within any reasoning-capable model, per-question position bias scales with the length of the reasoning trajectory.
  Across thirteen reasoning-mode configurations (two R1-distilled 7-8B models, two base models prompted with CoT, and DeepSeek-R1 at 671B) on MMLU, ARC-Challenge, and GPQA, twelve show a positive partial correlation between trajectory length and Position Bias Score (PBS) after controlling for accuracy, ranging from 0.11 to 0.41 (all p < 0.05). All twelve open-weight reasoning-mode configurations show monotonically increasing PBS across length quartiles. A truncation intervention provides causal evidence: continuations resumed from later points in the trajectory are increasingly likely to shift toward position-preferred options (16% to 32% for R1-Qwen-7B across absolute-position buckets).
  At 671B, aggregate PBS collapses to 0.019, but the length effect still manifests in the longest quartile (PBS = 0.071), suggesting that accuracy gates the expression of length-driven bias rather than eliminating the underlying mechanism. We additionally find that direct-answer position bias is a distinct phenomenon with a different footprint (strong in Llama-Instruct-direct, weak in Qwen-Instruct-direct, and uncorrelated with trajectory length): CoT reasoning replaces this baseline bias with length-accumulated bias.
  Our results argue that reasoning-capable models should not be treated as order-robust by default in MCQ evaluation pipelines, and offer a diagnostic toolkit (PBS, commitment change point, effective switching, truncation probes) for auditing position bias in reasoning models.

</details>


### [57] [Fast and Effective Redistricting Optimization via Composite-Move Tabu Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06682)
*Hai Jin,Diansheng Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种复合移动禁忌搜索算法（CM-Tabu），通过识别可同时移动或交换的单元集合（基于图的关节点和双连通分量分析），在保持空间邻接约束的同时系统性扩展可行邻域空间，显著提升了选区重划问题的求解质量、鲁棒性和计算效率，在费城案例中能够稳定达到人口均衡的理论全局最优解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 选区重划中的邻接约束是核心难点：传统整数规划和启发式搜索在强制邻接性时会严重压缩可行邻域空间，削弱探索能力，导致搜索陷入局部最优。现有方法缺乏在保持邻接前提下有效扩展邻域搜索范围的机制。

Method: 提出CM-Tabu算法，核心创新是复合移动操作：当单个边界单元无法在不破坏邻接性的前提下重新分配时，算法通过分析每个选区的邻接图（利用关节点和双连通分量），在线性时间内识别出可一起移动的最小单元集合，或可交换的单元对/集合对，作为保持邻接性的复合移动。这些复合移动与单单元移动共同构成候选移动集，集成到禁忌搜索框架中。

Result: 大量实验表明，CM-Tabu在求解质量、运行间鲁棒性和计算效率上显著优于传统禁忌搜索和其他基线方法。在费城案例中，该方法能够持续达到人口均衡的理论全局最优，并支持多目标权衡。

Conclusion: CM-Tabu为选区重划这一实际组合优化问题提供了适合真实实践和决策支持工作流的优化性能，通过系统性地扩展可行邻域空间，有效解决了邻接约束带来的搜索困境。

Abstract: Spatial redistricting is a practical combinatorial optimization problem that demands high-quality solutions, rapid turnaround, and flexibility to accommodate multi-criteria objectives and interactive refinement. A central challenge is the contiguity constraint: enforcing contiguity in integer-programming or heuristic search can severely shrink the feasible neighborhood, weaken exploration, and trap the search in poor local optima. We introduce a composite-move Tabu search (CM-Tabu) that systematically expands the feasible neighborhood space in Tabu search while preserving contiguity. When a boundary unit cannot be reassigned individually without disconnecting its district, our method identifies a minimal set of units that can move together, or a pair of units (or sets of units) that can be switched, as a contiguity-preserving composite move. Candidate single-unit and composite moves are generated in linear time by analyzing each district's contiguity graph using articulation points and biconnected components. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed approach substantially improves solution quality, run-to-run robustness, and computational efficiency relative to traditional Tabu search and other baselines. For example, in the Philadelphia case, the approach can consistently attain the theoretical global optimum in population-equality and support multi-criteria trade-offs. CM-Tabu delivers optimization performance suitable for real-world practices and decision-support workflows.

</details>


### [58] [State Representation and Termination for Recursive Reasoning Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06690)
*Debashis Guha,Amritendu Mukherjee,Sanjay Kukreja,Tarun Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出用认知状态图表示递归推理状态，并定义序隙（order-gap）作为停止迭代的判据，给出了该判据非退化的充要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归推理系统通常隐式地处理两个关键设计选择：如何表示演化的推理状态，以及何时停止迭代。本文旨在将这两个方面显式化。

Method: 将推理状态表示为认知状态图，包含提取的声明、证据关系、开放问题和置信权重。定义序隙为"先扩展后整合"与"先整合后扩展"两种策略所达状态之间的距离，并推导出线性化序隙在不动点附近非退化的充要条件。

Result: 给出了线性化序隙在不动点附近非退化的充要条件，表明该判据何时是有信息的而非代数上平凡。

Conclusion: 该框架适用于递归推理系统，并可推广到智能体循环、思维树推理、定理证明和持续学习等场景。但需注意这是一个局部条件，而非全局收敛保证。

Abstract: Recursive reasoning systems alternate between acquiring new evidence and refining an accumulated understanding. Two design choices are typically left implicit: how to represent the evolving reasoning state, and when to stop iterating. This paper addresses both. We represent the reasoning state as an epistemic state graph encoding extracted claims, evidential relations, open questions, and confidence weights. We define the order-gap as the distance between the states reached by expand-then-consolidate versus consolidate-then-expand; a small order-gap suggests that the two orderings agree and further iteration is unlikely to help. Our main result gives a necessary and sufficient condition for the linearised order-gap to be non-degenerate near the fixed point, showing when the criterion is informative rather than algebraically vacuous. This is a local condition, not a global convergence guarantee. We apply the framework to recursive reasoning systems and sketch its application to agent loops, tree-of-thought reasoning, theorem proving, and continual learning.

</details>


### [59] [Hidden Coalitions in Multi-Agent AI: A Spectral Diagnostic from Internal Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06696)
*Cameron Berg,Susan L. Schneider,Mark M. Bailey*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过分析多智能体系统内部神经表征的互信息图，并应用谱分割来检测联盟结构的方法。该方法在MARL环境和LLM提示场景中验证有效，能够区分真正的信息耦合与虚假的行为相似性，揭示出标量互信息无法分辨的群体组织层次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观察智能体行为不足以区分真正的信息耦合与虚假相似性，因为联盟可能在内部表征层面形成，而行为尚未显现变化。需要一种能从内部表征检测联盟结构的方法，以监控分布式AI系统中的涌现组织，这对AI安全与对齐至关重要。

Method: 从多智能体的隐藏状态构建成对互信息图，再应用谱分割识别最显著的联盟边界。在MARL环境中验证程序化层次结构和动态联盟结构的恢复能力及假阳性拒斥能力；在LLM场景中检测提示隐含的联盟结构、跟踪动态团队重组，并揭示表征层次。

Result: 在MARL环境中，方法成功恢复了预设的层次与动态联盟结构，正确拒绝了行为协调但不具备信息耦合的假阳性。在LLM场景中，方法识别出提示隐含的联盟结构，追踪了动态团队重新分配，并揭示了显式标签主导冲突交互模式的表征层次。两种设置下，谱分割恢复的子群组织是标量互信息无法区分的。

Conclusion: 通过谱分割分析隐藏状态互信息，提供了一种可扩展的诊断工具，用于识别表征层面的联盟结构，为监控分布式AI系统中的涌现群体组织提供了有效手段。

Abstract: Collections of interacting AI agents can form coalitions, creating emergent group-level organization that is critical for AI safety and alignment. However, observing agent behavior alone is often insufficient to distinguish genuine informational coupling from spurious similarity, as consequential coalitions may form at the level of internal representations before any overt behavioral change is apparent. Here, we introduce a practical method for detecting coalition structure from the internal neural representations of multi-agent systems. The approach constructs a pairwise mutual-information graph from the hidden states of agents and applies spectral partitioning to identify the most salient coalition boundary.
  We validate this method in two domains. First, in multi-agent reinforcement learning environments, the method successfully recovers programmed hierarchical and dynamic coalition structures and correctly rejects false positives arising from behavioral coordination without informational coupling. Second, using a large language model, the method identifies coalition structures implied by descriptive prompts, tracks dynamic team reassignments, and reveals a representational hierarchy where explicit labels dominate over conflicting interaction patterns. Across both settings, the recovered partition reveals subgroup organization that a scalar cross-agent mutual-information measure cannot distinguish. The results demonstrate that analyzing hidden-state mutual information through spectral partitioning provides a scalable diagnostic for identifying representational coalitions, offering a valuable tool for monitoring emergent structure in distributed AI systems.

</details>


### [60] [From Storage to Experience: A Survey on the Evolution of LLM Agent Memory Mechanisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06716)
*Jinghao Luo,Yuchen Tian,Chuxue Cao,Ziyang Luo,Hongzhan Lin,Kaixin Li,Chuyi Kong,Ruichao Yang,Jing Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个三阶段演化框架（存储→反思→经验）来统一LLM智能体记忆机制的研究，并分析了长程一致性、动态环境挑战和持续学习三大驱动力，为下一代智能体设计提供路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM智能体记忆机制的研究碎片化，分别从操作系统工程和认知科学两个角度出发，缺乏统一的技术综合视角和演化视角，因此需要弥合这一理论鸿沟。

Method: 提出了一个三阶段演化框架（存储→反思→经验），形式化定义每个阶段，分析三个核心驱动力（长程一致性需求、动态环境挑战、持续学习目标），并重点探讨经验阶段的两个变革机制（主动探索和跨轨迹抽象）。

Result: 提供了一个统一的演化视角来分析LLM智能体记忆机制，为不同研究方向提供了综合性的技术路线图。

Conclusion: 该框架为下一代LLM智能体记忆机制的设计提供了稳健的设计原则和清晰的发展路线图。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-based agents have fundamentally reshaped artificial intelligence by integrating external tools and planning capabilities. While memory mechanisms have emerged as the architectural cornerstone of these systems, current research remains fragmented, oscillating between operating system engineering and cognitive science. This theoretical divide prevents a unified view of technological synthesis and a coherent evolutionary perspective. To bridge this gap, this survey proposes a novel evolutionary framework for LLM agent memory mechanisms, formalizing the development process into three stages: Storage (trajectory preservation), Reflection (trajectory refinement), and Experience (trajectory abstraction). We first formally define these three stages before analyzing the three core drivers of this evolution: the necessity for long-range consistency, the challenges in dynamic environments, and the ultimate goal of continual learning. Furthermore, we specifically explore two transformative mechanisms in the frontier Experience stage: proactive exploration and cross-trajectory abstraction. By synthesizing these disparate views, this work offers robust design principles and a clear roadmap for the development of next-generation LLM agents.

</details>


### [61] [When Does a Language Model Commit? A Finite-Answer Theory of Pre-Verbalization Commitment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06723)
*Long Zhang,Wei-neng Chen,Feng-feng Wei,Zi-bo Qin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过有限答案偏好稳定化这一可计算对象，研究语言模型在生成最终答案前，其答案偏好何时变得稳定。在Qwen3-4B-Instruct上的延迟判决任务中，发现上下文有限答案投影在答案可解析之前就已稳定，领先17-31个token，且该信号追踪模型的最终输出而非事实真相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在给出最终答案前通常会生成推理过程，但可见答案无法揭示模型的答案偏好何时变得稳定。现有方法依赖贪婪解码或学习探针，缺乏精确、可计算的手段来定位这一稳定化时刻。

Method: 定义有限答案偏好稳定化这一可计算对象。将模型续写概率投影到有限答案集上，在二分类任务中得到精确的对数几率编码δ(ξ)=S_θ(yes|ξ)-S_θ(no|ξ)。据此定义基于解析器的答案起始时间、回顾性稳定化时间和领先量，无需依赖贪婪解码或学习探针。在Qwen3-4B-Instruct的延迟判决任务中进行受控实验。

Result: 上下文有限答案投影在答案可解析之前就已稳定，在主要模板中平均领先17-31个token；在解析器干净复现中领先量更短但仍然为正。该信号追踪模型最终输出而非事实，可从紧凑隐藏摘要中线性恢复，部分可分离于光标进度，作为共享信息传递而不存在单一不变坐标。

Conclusion: 有限答案偏好稳定化可作为研究模型内部决策时序的有效工具。诊断实验将其与在线停止、无口头词信念和因果答案控制区分开；精确操控显示δ局部敏感但不可靠地控制生成。

Abstract: Language models often generate reasoning before giving a final answer, but the visible answer does not reveal when the model's answer preference became stable. We study this question through a narrow computable object: \emph{finite-answer preference stabilization}. For a model state and specified answer verbalizers, we project the model's own continuation probabilities onto a finite answer set; in binary tasks this yields an exact log-odds code, $δ(ξ)=S_θ(\mathrm{yes}\midξ)-S_θ(\mathrm{no}\midξ)$. This target defines parser-based answer onset, retrospective stabilization time, and lead without relying on greedy rollouts or learned probes. In controlled delayed-verdict tasks with Qwen3-4B-Instruct, the contextual finite-answer projection stabilizes before the answer is parseable, with 17--31 token mean lead in the main templates and positive, shorter lead in a parser-clean replication. The signal tracks the model's eventual output rather than truth, is linearly recoverable from compact hidden summaries, is partly separable from cursor progress, and transfers as shared information without a single invariant coordinate. Diagnostics separate the measurement from online stopping, verbalizer-free belief, and causal answer control; exact steering shows local sensitivity of $δ$ but not reliable generation control.

</details>


### [62] [Towards Security-Auditable LLM Agents: A Unified Graph Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06812)
*Chaofan Li,Lyuye Zhang,Jintao Zhai,Siyue Feng,Xichun Yang,Huahao Wang,Shihan Dou,Yu Ji,Yutao Hu,Yueming Wu,Yang Liu,Deqing Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Agent-BOM，一种面向LLM智能体系统的统一结构化表示方法，通过分层属性有向图将静态能力基础与动态运行时语义状态分离，并利用图查询范式实现路径级风险评估，能够有效重建跨会话记忆投毒、工具滥用、供应链劫持等多种隐蔽攻击链。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体系统采用语义驱动的执行范式，导致底层物理事件与高层执行意图之间存在严重的语义鸿沟。静态SBOM和运行时日志等现有表示机制仅提供碎片化证据，无法捕获认知状态演化、能力绑定、持久性记忆污染以及跨智能体的级联风险传播，使得事后安全审计极为困难。

Method: 提出Agent-BOM，一种统一的结构化表示方法，将智能体系统建模为层次化属性有向图：(1) 分离静态能力基础（模型、工具、长期记忆）与动态运行时语义状态（目标、推理轨迹、动作）；(2) 通过语义边和安全属性连接各层，将碎片化执行轨迹转化为可查询的审计路径；(3) 基于图查询范式实现路径级风险评估，并基于OWASP Agentic Top 10进行实例化；(4) 在OpenClaw环境中实现审计插件，从实时执行中构建Agent-BOM。

Result: 在代表性真实世界智能体攻击场景上的评估表明，Agent-BOM能够有效重建多种隐蔽攻击链，包括：跨会话记忆投毒和工具滥用、能力供应链劫持和意外代码执行、多智能体生态系统劫持、以及权限与信任滥用。

Conclusion: Agent-BOM为复杂智能体生态系统中的根因分析和安全裁决提供了统一且可审计的基础，弥合了智能体安全审计中的语义鸿沟。

Abstract: LLM-based agentic systems are rapidly evolving to perform complex autonomous tasks through dynamic tool invocation, stateful memory management, and multi-agent collaboration. However, this semantics-driven execution paradigm creates a severe semantic gap between low-level physical events and high-level execution intent, making post-hoc security auditing fundamentally difficult. Existing representation mechanisms, including static SBOMs and runtime logs, provide only fragmented evidence and fail to capture cognitive-state evolution, capability bindings, persistent memory contamination, and cascading risk propagation across interacting agents. To bridge this gap, we propose Agent-BOM, a unified structural representation for agent security auditing. Agent-BOM models an agentic system as a hierarchical attributed directed graph that separates static capability bases, such as models, tools, and long-term memory, from dynamic runtime semantic states, such as goals, reasoning trajectories, and actions. These layers are connected through semantic edges and security attributes, transforming fragmented execution traces into queryable audit paths. Building on Agent-BOM, we develop a graph-query-based paradigm for path-level risk assessment and instantiate it with the OWASP Agentic Top 10. We further implement an auditing plugin in the OpenClaw environment to construct Agent-BOM from live executions. Evaluation on representative real-world agentic attack scenarios shows that Agent-BOM can reconstruct stealthy attack chains, including cross-session memory poisoning and tool misuse, capability supply-chain hijacking and unexpected code execution, multi-agent ecosystem hijacking, and privilege and trust abuse. These results demonstrate that Agent-BOM provides a unified and auditable foundation for root-cause analysis and security adjudication in complex agentic ecosystems.

</details>


### [63] [Uneven Evolution of Cognition Across Generations of Generative AI Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06815)
*Isaac Galatzer-Levy,Daniel McDuff,Xin Liu,Jed McGiffin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种心理测量框架来评估生成式AI的认知能力，通过对多模态模型的测试发现其认知架构极不平衡：言语理解和工作记忆接近天花板（>98百分位），而感知推理接近地板（<1百分位）。通过开发AIQ基准追踪六代模型，发现抽象数量推理在语言形式下发展更快，视觉感知组织能力停滞不前，表明当前缩放和优化方法可能不足以实现平衡的人类级通用智能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 追求通用人工智能需要超越狭隘任务性能的认知能力评估方法，因此需要建立一套能够将生成式AI与人类常模进行比较并追踪其跨代演化的稳健评估框架。

Method: 采用心理测量框架，使用改编自韦氏成人智力量表的任务对领先多模态模型进行初始评估，并开发了AIQ基准以追踪超越人类常模上限的发展轨迹，应用于两个模型家族的六代模型。

Result: 模型认知架构极不平衡：言语理解和工作记忆超过人类第98百分位，但感知推理低于第1百分位。抽象数量推理在语言形式下发展远快于视觉类比形式，视觉感知组织能力基本停滞。

Conclusion: 生成式AI的认知能力发展不均，当前的缩放和优化方法可能不足以克服基本架构限制，无法实现平衡的、类人的通用智能。

Abstract: The pursuit of artificial general intelligence necessitates robust methods for evaluating the cognitive capabilities of models beyond narrow task performance. Here, we introduce a psychometric framework to assess the cognitive profiles of generative AI, comparing them to human norms and tracking their evolution across generations. Initial evaluation of leading multimodal models using tasks adapted from the Wechsler Adult Intelligence Scale revealed a profoundly uneven cognitive architecture: near-ceiling performance in verbal comprehension and working memory (>$98^{\text{th}}$ percentile) contrasted with near-floor performance in perceptual reasoning (<$1^{\text{st}}$ percentile). To track developmental trajectories beyond human-normed limits, we developed the Artificial Intelligence Quotient (AIQ) Benchmark and applied it to six generations and two model families, revealing significant but asymmetric performance gains. Notably, we uncovered a sharp dissociation between modalities; abstract quantitative reasoning matured far more rapidly when presented linguistically compared to a visually analogous format, indicating an architectural bias towards language-based symbolic manipulation. While abstract visual reasoning improved, visual-perceptual organization remained largely stagnant. Collectively, these findings demonstrate that the cognitive abilities of generative models are evolving unevenly, suggesting that scaling and optimization approaches to AGI development alone may be insufficient to overcome fundamental architectural limitations in achieving balanced, human-like general intelligence.

</details>


### [64] [Extracting Search Trees from LLM Reasoning Traces Reveals Myopic Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06840)
*Sixing Chen,Ji-An Li,Saner Cakir,Sinan Akcali,Kayla Lee,Marcelo G. Mattar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过四子棋游戏提取LLM推理过程中的搜索树，发现LLM的规划比人类更浅，其决策主要由浅层节点驱动而非深层搜索，与人类规划模式形成鲜明对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM（尤其是推理模型）在思维链推理中常包含对未来结果的显式权衡，但这种权衡是否构成真正的规划、其结构如何、哪些方面驱动性能，目前尚不明确。

Method: 在四子棋游戏中从LLM推理轨迹中提取并量化搜索树，拟合计算模型分析规划结构，并通过因果干预实验（选择性剪枝CoT段落）验证决策驱动因素。

Result: LLM的搜索深度浅于人类；性能主要由搜索广度而非深度预测；LLM的决策最好由忽略深层节点的短视模型解释；因果干预证实决策主要由浅层节点驱动。

Conclusion: LLM与人类规划存在关键差异：人类专家的性能依赖深度搜索，而LLM并不基于深层前瞻行动。这为对齐LLM与人类规划提供了针对性指导，所提框架可推广至其他策略领域。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs), especially reasoning models, generate extended chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning that often contains explicit deliberation over future outcomes. Yet whether this deliberation constitutes genuine planning, how it is structured, and what aspects of it drive performance remain poorly understood. In this work, we introduce a new method to characterize LLM planning by extracting and quantifying search trees from reasoning traces in the four-in-a-row board game. By fitting computational models on the extracted search trees, we characterize how plans are structured and how they influence move decisions. We find that LLMs' search is shallower than humans', and that performance is predicted by search breadth rather than depth. Most strikingly, although LLMs expand deep nodes in their traces, their move choices are best explained by a myopic model that ignores those nodes entirely. A causal intervention study where we selectively prune CoT paragraphs further suggests that move selection is driven predominantly by shallow rather than deep nodes. These patterns contrast with human planning, where performance is driven primarily by deep search. Together, our findings reveal a key difference between LLM and human planning: while human expertise is driven by deeper search, LLMs do not act on deep lookahead. This dissociation offers targeted guidance for aligning LLM and human planning. More broadly, our framework provides a generalizable approach for interpreting the structure of LLM planning across strategic domains.

</details>


### [65] [AGWM: Affordance-Grounded World Models for Environments with Compositional Prerequisites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06841)
*Qinshi Zhang,Weipeng Deng,Zhihan Jiang,Jiaming Qu,Qianren Li,Weitao Xu,Ray LC*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对传统世界模型忽略动作先决条件、在多步预测中无法正确处理动作可执行性变化的问题，本文提出AGWM（Affordance-Grounded World Model），通过学习抽象的可供性结构（用DAG表示先决依赖关系）来显式追踪动作的动态可执行性，在游戏模拟环境中实现了更低的多步预测误差、更好的泛化能力和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于模型的学习中，标准世界模型学习的是静态的转移函数，倾向于将频繁共现的动作与结果内化为通用因果规则，而忽略了动作的先决条件（preconditions）。然而在交互式环境中，动作的可执行性是动态变化的（结构变化事件，SC events），当前时刻可执行的动作在下一时刻可能因先决条件被破坏而不可执行。传统模型在多步预测时，每一步都基于错误的可供性状态进行推断，导致误差随预测步长累积。

Method: 提出AGWM（Affordance-Grounded World Model），核心思想是学习一个抽象的可供性结构，用有向无环图（DAG）表示动作之间的先决依赖关系，显式地追踪每个动作在当前状态下是否可执行，从而在多步预测中准确判断动作的可执行性变化。

Result: 在基于游戏的模拟环境中，AGWM相比传统世界模型实现了更低的多步预测误差，对未见过的配置具有更好的泛化能力，同时具备更高的可解释性。

Conclusion: 显式建模动作的先决依赖关系和动态可供性状态，是提升世界模型在复杂交互环境中多步预测准确性和泛化能力的有效途径。

Abstract: In model-based learning, the agent learns behaviors by simulating trajectories based on world model predictions. Standard world models typically learn a stationary transition function that maps states and actions to next states, when an action and an outcome frequently co-occur in training data, the model tends to internalize this correlation as a general causal rule while ignoring action preconditions. In interactive environments, however, agent actions can reshape the future affordance space. At each timestep, an action may becomes executable only after its prerequisites are met, or non-executable when they are destroyed. We term such events structure-changing events (SC events). As a result, a conventional world model often fails to determine whether a given action is executable in the current state, especially in multi-step predictions. Each imagined step is conditioned on an incorrect affordance state, and therefore the prediction error compounds over the rollout horizon. In this paper, we propose AGWM (Affordance-Grounded World Model), which learns an abstract affordance structure represented as a DAG of prerequisite dependencies to explicitly track the dynamic executability of actions. Experiments on game-based simulated environments demonstrate the effectiveness of our method by achieving lower multi-step prediction error, better generalization to novel configurations, and improved interpretability.

</details>


### [66] [Agentick: A Unified Benchmark for General Sequential Decision-Making Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06869)
*Roger Creus Castanyer,Pablo Samuel Castro,Glen Berseth*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Agentick是一个用于公平比较RL、LLM、VLM、混合和人类智能体的统一基准，包含37个程序化生成任务、6种能力类别、4个难度级别和5种观测模态，通过27种配置和90,000+ episode的评估发现没有单一方法占优（GPT-5 mini以0.309归一化得分领先），推理框架可使LLM性能提升3-10倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体研究从RL到基础模型智能体跨度广泛，但缺乏统一的基准来公平比较这些不同方法。

Method: 构建了Agentick基准，提供37个程序化生成任务、6种能力类别、4个难度级别、5种观测模态，通过Gymnasium兼容接口、Coding API、oracle参考策略、SFT数据集和可组合智能体框架进行标准化评估。

Result: 27种配置、90,000+ episode评估显示：GPT-5 mini以0.309 oracle归一化得分总体领先；PPO在规划和多智能体任务中占优；推理框架使LLM性能提升3-10倍；ASCII观测一致优于自然语言。

Conclusion: 所有智能体范式仍有显著改进空间，Agentick的能力分解、多模态设计为通用自主智能体的发展提供了经验基础设施，既可作为评估框架，也可作为基础模型在真实序列环境中进行RL后训练的训练场。

Abstract: AI agent research spans a wide spectrum: from RL agents that learn from scratch to foundation model agents that leverage pre-trained knowledge, yet no unified benchmark enables fair comparison across these approaches. We present Agentick, a benchmark for sequential decision-making agents designed to evaluate RL, LLM, VLM, hybrid, and human agents on common ground and to power research on the fundamental challenges of sequential decision-making. Agentick provides 37 procedurally generated tasks across six capability categories, four difficulty levels, and five observation modalities, all exposed through a single Gymnasium-compatible interface. The benchmark ships with a Coding API, oracle reference policies for all tasks, pre-built SFT datasets, a composable agent harness, and a live leaderboard. An evaluation spanning 27 configurations and over 90,000 episodes reveals that no single approach dominates: GPT-5 mini leads overall at 0.309 oracle-normalized score while PPO dominates planning and multi-agent tasks; the reasoning harness multiplies LLM performance by 3-10x; and ASCII observations consistently outperform natural language. These findings highlight the substantial room for improvement that remains across all agent paradigms. Agentick's capability-decomposed, multi-modal design provides the empirical infrastructure needed to drive progress toward general autonomous agents, both as an evaluation framework and as a training ground for RL post-training of foundation models in truly sequential environments.

</details>


### [67] [How Well Do LLMs Perform on the Simplest Long-Chain Reasoning Tasks: An Empirical Study on the Equivalence Class Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06882)
*Chun Zheng,Lianlong Wu,Bingqian Li,Lvting Liu,Yi Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 这篇论文评估了大型语言模型（包括非推理模型和推理模型）在等价类问题（ECP）上的表现。实验发现：非推理模型完全无法解决ECP，而推理模型表现更好但仍不能完全解决。有趣的是，非推理模型的最难点出现在相变点ln n/(n-1)附近，而推理模型的最难点出现在图直径最大的时候。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLMs近年来取得了巨大进步，但它们在推理任务（尤其是长链推理）上的能力仍不明确。作者希望通过最简单的长链推理任务——等价类问题来系统评估LLMs的推理能力。

Method: 通过构造随机生成的等价关系集合，定义等价类问题（ECP），并系统性地变化变量数量、连接概率、提示方式等因素，对非推理型和推理型两类代表性LLMs进行大规模实验评估。

Result: 非推理LLMs完全无法解决ECP；推理模型显著更好但仍不能完全解决。非推理模型的最难实例出现在连接概率为ln n/(n-1)的相变点，而推理模型的最难实例出现在图直径最大的时候。

Conclusion: 非推理模型和推理模型在ECP上的表现差异揭示了它们不同的失败模式：非推理模型受问题的混沌程度（相变点）影响，而推理模型受推理链的长度（图直径）影响。这说明当前LLMs在长链推理任务上仍有明显不足。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have achieved great improvements in recent years. Nevertheless, it still remains unclear how good LLMs are for reasoning tasks, especially for long-chain ones. In this paper, we evaluate LLMs' performance on the simplest yet long-chain reasoning task, namely the Equivalence Class Problem (ECP), i.e., determining whether two variables are equal given a set of randomly generated equivalence relations. We consider both reasoning and non-reasoning representative LLMs over a large variety of problem instances, ranging over different numbers of variables, connectivity probabilities, prompts, and other factors. The experimental results show that non-reasoning LLMs fail ECP, while reasoning models are significantly better but still struggle to completely solve this problem. Interestingly, considering various connectivity probabilities with a fixed number of variables, we observe that, for non-reasoning models, the hardest problem instances coincide with the phase transition point of ln n/(n-1), suggesting the chaos of the problem; in contrast, for reasoning models, the hardest ones coincide with the biggest diameter, suggesting the reasoning difficulty of the problem.

</details>


### [68] [Beyond the Black Box: Interpretability of Agentic AI Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06890)
*Hariom Tatsat,Ariye Shater*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套基于稀疏自编码器（SAEs）和线性探针的机制可解释性工具包，通过读取模型在行动前的内部状态，推断是否需要调用工具以及下一步工具操作的后果严重性，从而为AI智能体的工具调用提供内部可观测性，尤其适用于长期高风险的企业工作流场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体在高风险企业工作流中部署受限，因为工具调用错误难以诊断和控制。现有可观测性方法（提示词、评估分数、日志）都是外部视角，无法在行动前感知模型内部状态，尤其在长周期任务中，早期工具错误会改变后续轨迹、增加token消耗并带来安全风险。

Method: 使用稀疏自编码器（SAEs）和线性探针构建机制可解释性工具包。在每次行动前读取模型状态，推断是否需要工具以及工具操作的后果严重性。通过将激活分解为稀疏特征，识别与工具决策最相关的内部层和特征，并通过特征消融测试其功能重要性。在NVIDIA Nemotron函数调用数据集的多步轨迹上训练探针，并应用于GPT-OSS 20B和Gemma 3 27B模型。

Result: 成功识别出模型内部与工具决策相关的层和特征，通过特征消融验证了其功能重要性。该工具包提供了行动前的内部信号可见性，能揭示智能体故障的深层原因，特别是在长周期任务中早期错误的级联影响。

Conclusion: 该工作并非取代外部评估，而是增加缺失的内部可观测性层，帮助在智能体行动前理解其内部信号。展示了机制可解释性如何为智能体系统的工具调用监控和风险管理提供实用的内部可观测性支持。

Abstract: AI agents are promising for high-stakes enterprise workflows, but dependable deployment remains limited because tool-use failures are difficult to diagnose and control. Agents may skip required tool calls, invoke tools unnecessarily, or take actions whose consequence becomes visible only after execution. Existing observability methods are mostly external: prompts reveal correlations, evaluations score outputs, and logs arrive only after the model has already acted. In long-horizon settings, these failures are especially costly because an early tool mistake can alter the rest of the trajectory, increase token consumption, and create downstream safety and security risk.
  We introduce a mechanistic-interpretability toolkit built on Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) and linear probes. The framework reads model states before each action and infers both whether a tool is needed and how consequential the next tool action is likely to be. By decomposing activations into sparse features, it identifies the internal layers and features most associated with tool decisions and tests their functional importance through feature ablation. We train the probes on multi-step trajectories from the NVIDIA Nemotron function-calling dataset and apply the same workflow to GPT-OSS 20B and Gemma 3 27B models.
  The goal is not to replace external evaluation, but to add a missing layer: visibility into what the model signaled internally before action. This helps surface deeper causes of agent failure, especially in long-horizon runs where an early mistake can reshape the rest of the agentic interaction. More broadly, the paper shows how mechanistic interpretability can support practical internal observability for monitoring tool calls and risk in agent systems.

</details>


### [69] [Mitigating Cognitive Bias in RLHF by Altering Rationality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06895)
*Tiffany Horter,Andrew Markham,Niki Trigoni,Serena Booth*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种动态调整理性参数β的方法，利用LLM作为裁判评估人类反馈中认知偏差的存在，在奖励学习过程中降低不可靠比较的权重，从而训练出更鲁棒的强化学习模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RLHF中假设人类反馈服从固定的理性参数β，但真实人类判断受认知偏差影响，存在系统性偏离理性行为的问题，需要一种能适应上下文和标注者差异的鲁棒方法。

Method: 将理性参数β视为上下文和标注相关的动态变量，使用LLM作为裁判评估认知偏差的存在，动态调整β值以降低有偏或不可靠比较的权重。

Result: 在强偏差偏好的数据集上进行微调时，该方法仍能学习到更理性的下游模型，经验上验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: 通过动态调整理性参数可以有效缓解人类反馈中的认知偏差问题，使RLHF对不完美的人类反馈更加鲁棒。

Abstract: How can we make models robust to even imperfect human feedback? In reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF), human preferences over model outputs are used to train a reward model that assigns scalar values to responses. Because these rewards are inferred from pairwise comparisons, this learning depends on an assumed relationship between latent reward differences and observed preferences, typically modeled using a Boltzmann formulation in which a rationality parameter beta informs how consistently preferences reflect reward differences. In practice, beta is typically treated as a fixed constant that reflects assumed uniform annotator reliability. However, human feedback is not this simplistic in practice: real human judgments are shaped by cognitive biases, leading to systematic deviations from reward-consistent behavior that arise contextually. To address this, we treat rationality as context- and annotation-dependent. We design an approach to dynamically adjust the rationality parameter beta during reward learning using an LLM-as-judge to assess the likely presence of cognitive biases. This approach effectively downweights comparisons that are likely to reflect biased or unreliable judgments. Empirically, we show that this approach learns a more rational downstream model, even when finetuning on datasets with strongly biased preferences.

</details>


### [70] [Self-Programmed Execution for Language-Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06898)
*Luke J. O'Connor*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出自编程执行（SPE）架构，让语言模型完成本身作为编排程序，通过基于Lisp的Spell语言实现自编辑和自求值，使前沿模型无需固定编排策略即可完成复杂智能体任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型智能体依赖固定的编排程序来管理回合间状态转换，这种固定策略限制了模型的自主性和灵活性。作者希望打破这一限制，让模型自身成为编排者。

Method: 提出SPE架构，核心思想是模型完成本身即编排程序。形式化为"智能体机器"概念：SPE状态中，模型完成可加载嵌入式副本的任何状态。引入Spell语言（基于Lisp），支持程序自编辑和自求值，且效果表达式（如模型调用）在重求值时不会重放副作用。

Result: 实验表明，未针对SPE或Spell训练的前沿模型也能在该框架下运行，完成具有挑战性的智能体任务。

Conclusion: 证明了语言模型可以在没有任何固定编排策略的情况下作为智能体运行，并提出了一个开放问题：经过SPE训练的模型可能学到什么样的自编排策略。

Abstract: At the heart of existing language model agents is a fixed orchestrator program responsible for the state transition between consecutive turns. This paper introduces self-programmed execution (SPE), an agent architecture in which the model completion is itself the orchestrator program, and the harness evaluates this program but does not impose its own orchestration policy. I formalize this idea using agentic machines: an SPE state is one from which a model completion can load any state of an embedded copy of the machine, meaning that it is subject to no fixed turn-to-turn orchestration policy. Realizing SPE in practice is nontrivial because the same data is both model context and executable program. I therefore introduce Spell, a Lisp-based language in which programs can edit and re-evaluate themselves, and effectful expressions like model invocations are structured such that re-evaluating an edited program does not replay its side effects. Experiments with existing models, not trained for SPE or Spell, show that frontier models can operate in this regime and accomplish challenging agentic tasks. These results demonstrate how an LM can act as an agent without any fixed orchestration policy, and they raise the question of what self-orchestration strategies might be learned by a model trained for self-programmed execution. Code is available at https://github.com/lukejoconnor/spell .

</details>


### [71] [Multi-Objective Constraint Inference using Inverse reinforcement learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06951)
*Syed Ihtesham Hussain Shah,Floris den Hengst,Aneta Lisowska,Annette ten Teije*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出多目标约束推断框架MOCI，从异构专家轨迹中同时提取共享约束和个体偏好，在网格世界基准测试中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有约束推断方法通常假设专家演示是同质的（单一专家或目标相同的多个专家），难以捕捉个体偏好且计算效率低下，无法处理多专家追求不同目标的异构场景。

Method: 提出MOCI框架，从异构专家轨迹中联合推断共享约束和个体偏好，建模并学习多样化甚至相互冲突的行为模式。

Result: 在标准网格世界基准测试中，MOCI显著优于现有基线方法，具备更优的预测性能和具有竞争力的计算效率。

Conclusion: MOCI是一种准确、灵活且计算实用的方法，适用于现实世界的约束推断和偏好学习任务。

Abstract: Constraint inference is widely considered essential to align reinforcement learning agents with safety boundaries and operational guidelines by observing expert demonstrations. However, existing approaches typically assume homogeneous demonstrations (i.e., generated by a single expert or multiple experts with identical objectives). They also have limited ability to capture individual preferences and often suffer from computational inefficiencies. In this paper, we introduce Multi-Objective Constraint Inference (MOCI), a novel framework designed to jointly extract shared constraints and individual preferences from heterogeneous expert trajectories, where multiple experts pursue different objectives. MOCI effectively models and learns from diverse, and potentially conflicting, behaviors. Empirical evaluations demonstrate that MOCI significantly outperforms existing baselines, achieving improved predictive performance, and maintaining competitive computational efficiency on a standard grid-world benchmark. These results establish MOCI as an accurate, flexible, and computationally practical approach for real-world constraint inference and preference learning tasks.

</details>


### [72] [Adaptive auditing of AI systems with anytime-valid guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07002)
*Siyu Zhou,Patrick Vossler,Venkatesh Sivaraman,Yifan Mai,Jean Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对生成式AI系统自适应测试中统计推断不严谨的问题，提出了一种基于安全任意时间有效推断（SAVI）的对赌假设检验框架，从模型和审计者双重视角构建竞争性零假设，通过e-过程实现任意时间有效的错误控制，理论上证明强审计可通过全局鲁棒性认证，实验表明仅需20个观测值即可得出统计严谨结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI系统失效模式标注成本高昂，自适应测试范式虽灵活但违反了经典统计假设（观测数有限且采样/停止决策在数据收集过程中而非预先指定），导致难以得出统计严谨的结论。现有方法缺乏在高度自适应审计下进行有效统计推断的理论框架。

Method: 1. 构建双竞争零假设框架：模型零假设（无低于目标阈值的失效模式）与审计者零假设（存在能发现失效模式的采样策略）。2. 基于SAVI（安全任意时间有效推断）将审计形式化为"对赌检验"，转化为同时检验双零假设的e-过程。3. 理论证明当审计者足够强大时，两个假设渐近互逆，严格审计可通过全局鲁棒性认证。

Result: 1. 所提检验程序能维持任意时间有效的第一类错误控制。2. 性能优于预先指定的检验方法。3. 在低至20个观测值时即可得出统计严谨的结论。

Conclusion: 该工作为高度自适应的AI审计提供了统计严谨的理论框架和方法工具，弥合了自适应测试实用性与统计推断严谨性之间的鸿沟，使得在极小样本下也能对生成式AI系统的失效模式进行可靠的统计推断。

Abstract: A major bottleneck in characterizing the failure modes of generative AI systems is the cost and time of annotation and evaluation. Consequently, adaptive testing paradigms have gained popularity, where one opportunistically decides which cases and how many to annotate based on past results. While this framework is highly practical, its extreme flexibility makes it difficult to draw statistically rigorous conclusions, as it violates classical assumptions: the number of observations is typically limited (often 10 to 50 cases) and decisions regarding sampling and stopping are made in the midst of data collection rather than based a pre-specified rule.
