Under mild conditions, we prove highprobability bound on value estimates in POMDPs with continuous observation space. Specifically, we propose Voro-POMCPOW, a variant of POMCPOW with f inite-time guarantees that adaptively partitions the continuous observation space using Voronoi cells. This approach maintains a finite branching factor while preserving the original observation generator. Empirical validation demonstrates that the proposed Voro-POMCPOW shows competitive performance while providing theoretical guarantees. Although our analysis focuses on continuous POMDPs, the techniques developed herein are also applicable to continuous MDPs, closing another gap on the MDP side.

</details>


### [110] [Online Goal Recognition using Path Signature and Dynamic Time Warping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07736)
*Douglas Tesch,Nathan Gavenski,Leonardo Amado,Odinaldo Rodrigues,Felipe Meneguzzi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用路径签名（path signatures）进行在线目标识别的新方法，该方法能紧凑且富有表现力地表示轨迹，在预测准确性和在线规划效率上持续超越现有最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线目标识别面临两大挑战：高效编码长轨迹以及有效比较轨迹。现有方法使用自定义状态空间表示和度量，忽略了其他领域成熟的编码技术。

Method: 引入粗糙路径理论中的路径签名作为轨迹的紧凑表达，高效捕获轨迹的关键语义特征，实现更有意义的轨迹比较。

Result: 在预测准确性和在线规划效率上持续超越现有最优方法，在离线场景下也保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 路径签名是一种有效的在线目标识别工具，兼顾了表达能力和计算效率。

Abstract: Online goal recognition in continuous domains poses two central challenges: efficiently encoding large trajectories and effectively comparing them. Recent work addresses these challenges by using custom state-space representations and metrics to compare observations against hypotheses. However, these approaches often overlook well-established encoding techniques used in other domains that offer substantial advantages. This paper introduces a novel method for online goal recognition that leverages path signatures, a compact, expressive representation of rough path theory that efficiently captures key semantic features of trajectories, enabling more meaningful comparisons between them. Experiments show that our method consistently outperforms the state of the art in predictive accuracy and online planning efficiency, while remaining competitive offline.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [111] [Simplicial Regularizability of the Pseudo-Moment Cone and Carathéodory-Type Atomic Decomposition of Moment Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06854)
*Shucheng Kang,Heng Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了齐次伪矩锥Σ_{n,2d}^*的面几何及其对矩矩阵原子分解的意义。证明了当矩矩阵由O(n^d)个一般选择的加权原子构成时，其在伪矩锥矩阵实现中的最小面是单纯形面并由植入的秩一原子生成。基于此几何结果，提出了谱面锥的Carathéodory型极射线分解算法，并特化到伪矩锥以实现矩矩阵的原子分解。数值实验验证了算法的恢复性能，并表明在保证区域外算法可作为高秩极射线的实用采样器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索伪矩锥的面几何结构，以理解矩矩阵原子分解的几何本质，并基于此设计高效的分解算法。

Method: 首先建立伪矩锥中矩矩阵最小面的单纯形性质与生成元（秩一原子）的对应关系；然后基于该几何结果，提出适用于谱面锥的Carathéodory型极射线分解算法；最后特化到伪矩锥场景实现原子分解。

Result: 证明了在O(n^d)个一般选择加权原子条件下，矩矩阵的最小面是单纯形面；提出的算法在该条件下能高效恢复原子分解；数值实验展示了强恢复性能，且在保证区域外可作为高秩极射线采样器。

Conclusion: 伪矩锥的面几何为矩矩阵原子分解提供了严格的几何框架，所提算法在理论和实践上均有效，且具有超出理论保证范围的实用潜力。

Abstract: We study the facial geometry of the homogeneous pseudo-moment cone \(Σ_{n,2d}^*\) and its implications for atomic decomposition of moment matrices. For fixed \(d \ge 2\), we show that if a moment matrix is formed by \(O(n^d)\) generically chosen weighted atoms, then its minimal face in the matrix realization of the pseudo-moment cone is \emph{simplicial} and generated by the planted rank-one atoms. Based on this geometric result, we develop a Carathéodory-type extreme-ray decomposition algorithm for spectrahedral cones and show that, when specialized to the pseudo-moment cone, it yields an efficient atomic decomposition method for generically generated moment matrices in the same regime. A stabilized numerical implementation demonstrates strong recovery performance and suggests that, outside the guaranteed regime, the algorithm may serve as a practical sampler of high-rank extreme rays.

</details>


### [112] [Muon with Nesterov Momentum: Heavy-Tailed Noise and (Randomized) Inexact Polar Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06884)
*Sayantan Choudhury,Xiaoran Cheng,Martin Takáč,Sen Na,Mladen Kolar*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文为Muon优化器（含Nesterov动量、不精确极分解）在非凸矩阵优化及重尾噪声下建立了收敛理论，证明了最优迭代与样本复杂度，并分析了随机低秩极分解的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon优化器在实践中通过极分解处理矩阵参数的几何结构，但其理论基础薄弱：实际实现包含Nesterov动量、近似极分解及重尾随机梯度，这些因素缺乏严格的理论分析。

Method: 构建了不精确极分解的统一分析框架（涵盖Newton-Schulz等迭代近似方法），量化近似误差在优化动态中的传播；在此基础上推导了Muon在重尾噪声下的收敛性与复杂度；分析了随机低秩极分解的算法和理论保证。

Result: 建立了Muon达到ε-驻点时的最优迭代和样本复杂度为O(ε^{-(3α-2)/(α-1)})，其中α∈(1,2]为重尾指数；在σ₁=0的不精确极分解设置下，给出了无需先验α知识的收敛保证；随机低秩分解在保持理论兼容性的同时显著提升效率。

Conclusion: 为含Nesterov动量与不精确极分解的Muon提供了完备的收敛理论，填补了实践与理论之间的鸿沟，并提出了高效的随机低秩变体，实验验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Most first-order optimizers treat matrix-valued parameters as vectors, ignoring the intrinsic geometry of hidden-layer weights in neural networks. Muon addresses this mismatch by updating along the polar factor of a momentum matrix, but its theoretical understanding has lagged behind practice. In particular, practical implementations incorporate Nesterov momentum, compute the polar factor only approximately, and operate with stochastic gradients that may be heavy-tailed. We close this gap by developing a convergence theory for Muon with Nesterov momentum and inexact polar decomposition in non-convex matrix optimization under heavy-tailed noise. Our analysis builds on a unified framework for inexact polar decomposition that captures practical iterative approximations such as Newton-Schulz and quantifies how their errors propagate through the optimization dynamics. Under this framework, we establish an optimal iteration and sample complexity of $O \left(\varepsilon^{\frac{-(3α-2)}{(α-1)}} \right)$ for finding an $\varepsilon$-stationary point, where $α\in(1,2]$ denotes the heavy-tail index. For the inexact-polar setting with $σ_1=0$, we also provide guarantees that do not require prior knowledge of $α$. We analyze a randomized low-rank polar decomposition that is substantially more efficient than full-space methods while remaining compatible with our theory. Numerical experiments further demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed inexact and randomized variants.

</details>


### [113] [Prescriptive Optimization for Adaptive Auto-insurance Pricing with Telematics Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06954)
*Qinyang He,Yonatan Mintz*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于最优控制框架的按里程计费保险（UBI）动态定价方法，通过拉格朗日松弛分解非凸的集中式问题，理论证明对偶间隙随组合规模扩大而消失，实现渐近最优性，并在模拟工业级组合上验证了计算可行性和优于静态基线的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UBI研究主要聚焦于驾驶行为画像（profiling），而缺乏基于数据的定价策略（prescriptive pricing）探索。同时，部署UBI面临赔付成本重尾分布、驾驶行为非平稳性以及激励预算有限等挑战。

Method: 提出一个最优控制框架，整合远程信息处理数据用于动态定价。具体包括：（i）学习索赔频率和严重程度；（ii）建模驾驶员在折扣影响下的多周期行为演化；（iii）使用拉格朗日松弛优化整个投资组合的折扣分配，将非凸集中问题分解为独立的动态系统。

Result: 理论证明了拉格朗日松弛的对偶间隙随投资组合规模扩大而消失，保证了渐近最优性。在模拟工业级组合上的计算实验表明，该方法在计算上可行，且优于静态基线方法，能够同时降低预期损失和索赔概率。

Conclusion: 该动态定价框架能同时惠及保险公司（降低预期损失）和投保人（降低索赔概率），为UBI的规模化部署提供了理论保证和实用工具。

Abstract: Usage-based insurance (UBI) uses telematics to align premiums with risk and encourage safe driving. However, deploying these programs is challenging due to heavy-tailed claim costs, nonstationary driver behavior, and limited incentive budgets. While existing research focuses on profiling drivers, prescriptive pricing remains underexplored. We propose an optimal control framework that integrates telematics directly into dynamic pricing. Our approach (i) learns claim frequency and severity, (ii) models multi-period behavioral evolution in response to discounts, and (iii) optimizes portfolio-wide discount allocation using a Lagrangian relaxation. This decomposes the non-convex centralized problem into independent dynamical systems. We theoretically prove this relaxation's duality gap vanishes as the portfolio scales, guaranteeing asymptotic optimality. We validate our approach computationally on a simulated industry-scale portfolio. Our results demonstrate not only the computational tractability of our approach but also that it outperforms static baselines, reducing both expected losses and claim probabilities to benefit insurers and policyholders alike.

</details>


### [114] [Unlocking Deep Demand Flexibility via Dynamic Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06960)
*Xinyang Zhou,Jing Shang,Andrey Bernstein,Stefan Wager,Moody Saleh,Lara Pierpoint*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种隐私保护的动态定价框架，通过反馈学习算法更新日前电价，解决静态TOU tariff下HEMS设备同步导致的电网稳定性问题。在84节点配电网仿真中验证，该方法能显著降低峰值负荷和负荷波动，在高可再生能源渗透率下效果更佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态TOU电价下，高度自动化的家庭能源管理系统（HEMS）会因同时响应而引发"设备同步"现象，产生人为需求尖峰，威胁电网稳定性。因此需要一种既能挖掘灵活性又保护隐私的定价机制。

Method: 提出一种隐私保护的、单向动态信号框架。利用基于反馈的学习算法，根据变电站聚合需求和环境上下文更新日前电价曲线，实现电网目标与边缘聚合行为之间的闭环控制。

Result: 在84节点配电网、数百个HEMS（含HVAC、光伏、电池、柔性负荷）的高保真仿真中，该方法显著削减了峰值需求和总负荷波动。跨气候和规模的可扩展性分析表明动态定价是DERs的"力量倍增器"，高可再生能源渗透率下削峰潜力大幅提升。

Conclusion: 提出的隐私保护动态定价框架能够有效解锁HEMS的深度需求灵活性，缓解设备同步问题，且在多种环境条件下具有鲁棒性，为高DER渗透率下的电网稳定运行提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of distributed energy resources (DERs) and the electrification of residential loads offer significant potential for grid flexibility but pose stability challenges under static pricing regimes. Specifically, high levels of automation under static Time-of-Use (TOU) tariffs often induce ``device synchronization,'' where simultaneous responses from home energy management systems (HEMS) create artificial demand peaks that threaten grid stability. This paper proposes a privacy-preserving, one-way dynamic signaling framework to unlock deep demand flexibility from HEMS. We utilize a feedback-based learning algorithm that updates day-ahead price profiles based on aggregate substation demand and environmental contexts, effectively closing the loop between utility objectives and aggregated edge behaviors. The framework is rigorously validated using high-fidelity simulations on an 84-bus distribution network populated with hundreds of HEMS controlling diverse devices, including HVAC, PV, batteries, and flexible loads. Results demonstrate that the proposed mechanism achieves substantial reductions in both peak demand and total load variation. Extensive analyses across diverse climates and scalable deployments confirm the framework's robustness, indicating that dynamic pricing acts as a force multiplier for DERs, with peak shaving potential increasing significantly under high renewable penetration scenarios.

</details>


### [115] [A Semi-smooth Newton Method for the Constrained Optimal Control of Continuous-Time Linear Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06991)
*Simon J. Jones,Dominic Liao-McPherson,Marco M. Nicotra*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的间接方法，通过在连续时间函数空间中嵌入KKT条件到非光滑互补函数，将其转化为Banach空间上的求根问题，并利用非光滑牛顿法求解，其中牛顿更新通过求解修正的微分Riccati方程获得。数值实验表明方法具有超线性收敛速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有约束最优控制问题的间接方法通常面临互补条件的非光滑性带来的求解困难，需要一种能够在连续时间函数空间中直接处理KKT条件且具有良好收敛特性的数值方法。

Method: 1) 将KKT条件嵌入非光滑互补函数，重构成Banach空间上的求根问题；2) 采用非光滑牛顿法求解该问题；3) 牛顿更新步通过求解一个修正的微分Riccati方程得到，其中每次迭代根据约束乘子重新加权成本项。

Result: 数值仿真表明该方法有效，收敛速度达到超线性，收敛精度达到ODE求解器的容忍度限制。

Conclusion: 该方法成功在连续时间函数空间中直接求解约束最优控制问题，兼具间接法的解析优势与牛顿法的快速收敛特性。

Abstract: This paper details a novel indirect method for solving constrained optimal control problems (OCPs) directly in continuous-time function space. The KKT conditions are embedded in a non-smooth complementarity function, which enables their reformulation as a rootfinding problem in Banach space. This problem is then solved using a non-smooth Newton method. Finally, the paper shows that the Newton update can be obtained by solving a modified differential Riccati equation, where the cost terms are reweighted at every iteration based on the constraint multipliers. Numerical simulations show the effectiveness of the method, which converges superlinearly up to the tolerance of the ODE solver.

</details>


### [116] [Lectures on optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07006)
*Sinho Chewi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 这些讲义涵盖了凸优化理论，特别关注一阶方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 凸优化在机器学习、信号处理、控制理论等领域有广泛应用，需系统化理论梳理。

Method: 采用讲义形式，系统阐述凸优化的理论基础，重点分析一阶优化方法的数学原理与收敛性。

Result: 提供了凸优化理论尤其是梯度下降、加速梯度等一阶方法的完整数学框架。

Conclusion: 这些讲义为理解凸优化及一阶方法提供了严谨的理论基础。

Abstract: These lecture notes cover the theory of convex optimization, with a particular emphasis on first-order methods.

</details>


### [117] [Cross-validation-based optimal feature selection for linear SVM classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07089)
*Masaharu Mori,Shunnosuke Ikeda,Ryuta Tamura,Yuichi Takano,Ryuhei Miyashiro*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于交叉验证准则的最小二乘支持向量机（LS-SVM）特征子集选择方法，通过将双层混合整数优化问题简化为单层混合整数优化问题，实现了在分类问题中同时优化分类精度和特征选择准确性。实验表明该方法在分类精度和特征选择准确性上优于L1正则化、递归特征消除和基于统计准则的混合整数优化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交叉验证准则相比AIC、BIC等统计准则仅需样本独立同分布的弱假设，适用范围更广且在回归问题中已展现出有效性，但尚未被系统应用于SVM分类问题的特征子集选择。

Method: 将特征子集选择问题形式化为双层混合整数优化问题，利用最小二乘支持向量机（LS-SVM）最优性条件可闭式表达的特性，将问题简化为单层混合整数优化问题，从而可用标准优化软件求解。

Result: 通过仿真实验与L1正则化、递归特征消除（RFE）和基于统计准则的混合整数优化方法比较，所提框架在分类准确度和特征选择准确度两方面均取得更优性能。

Conclusion: 基于交叉验证准则的LS-SVM特征子集选择方法有效且性能优越，为解决SVM分类中的特征选择问题提供了新的可行途径。

Abstract: This paper addresses feature subset selection for Support Vector Machines (SVMs) based on the cross-validation criterion. Unlike statistical criteria such as the Akaike information criterion (AIC) and the Bayesian information criterion (BIC), cross-validation requires only the mild assumption that samples are independently and identically distributed (i.i.d.). For this reason, the cross-validation criterion is expected to work well across a wide range of prediction problems, and it has already demonstrated its usefulness as a feature subset selection method for regression. The objective of this paper is to extend the framework of best feature subset selection via the cross-validation criterion to SVM classification problems. This subset-selection problem can be formulated as a bilevel mixed-integer optimization problem. Because bilevel optimization problems are generally hard to solve, we introduce the Least Squares Support Vector Machine (LS-SVM), whose optimality conditions admit a closed-form expression, and reduce the problem to a single-level mixed-integer optimization problem. This reformulation allows us to solve the problem using standard optimization software. We evaluate the proposed framework through simulation experiments that compare it with a regularization-based method (L1-regularization), a sequential search method (recursive feature elimination), and mixed-integer optimization (MIO) based on statistical criteria. The results show that the proposed framework achieves favorable performance both in classification accuracy and feature selection accuracy.

</details>


### [118] [Prox-PEP: A Proximal Partial Exact Penalty Algorithm for Weakly Convex Stochastic Nonlinear Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07165)
*Lixin Tang,Xingyu Wang,Liwei Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出Prox-PEP算法，一种针对弱凸随机优化问题的近端方法，通过精确罚函数处理等式约束，采用动态罚参数策略，实现了关于ε-KKT平稳性的O(T^{-1/4})平均期望复杂度以及O(T^{-1/8})的高概率收敛界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机优化方法在处理同时具有弱凸目标函数和非线性约束的随机优化问题时，缺乏有效的收敛性保证。本文旨在填补这一理论空白，为带有非线性等式约束的弱凸随机优化问题设计具有严格收敛保证的算法。

Method: 提出Prox-PEP算法：(1) 采用精确罚函数将非线性等式约束转化为带有辅助松弛变量的不等式约束；(2) 每次迭代构造目标和约束函数的二次近似，精心设计二阶近似矩阵以保证增广拉格朗日子问题的强凸性；(3) 采用动态等式罚参数策略，单调递增至预设阈值后保持恒定。

Result: 证明了Prox-PEP的渐近复杂度：(1) ε-KKT平稳性上平均期望复杂度为O(T^{-1/4})；(2) 在轻尾鞅差噪声假设下，拉格朗日函数Moreau包络梯度的范数的高概率收敛界为O(T^{-1/8})，约束违反和互补条件的高概率界为O(T^{-1/4})。

Conclusion: Prox-PEP为带有非线性等式约束的弱凸随机优化问题提供了首个具有完整收敛性保证的近端算法，其复杂度分析涵盖了期望意义和高概率意义下的收敛结果，为该类问题提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: This paper considers stochastic optimization problems with weakly convex objective and constraint functions. We propose Prox-PEP, a proximal method equipped with quadratic subproblems. To handle nonlinear equality constraints, we employ an exact penalty approach, transforming them into inequality constraints with auxiliary slack variables. At each iteration, we construct quadratic approximations for both the objective and the constraint functions to facilitate efficient subproblem computation. By carefully designing the second-order approximation matrices, the subproblem constructed via the augmented Lagrangian function is strictly guaranteed to be strongly convex. Furthermore, we adopt a dynamic strategy for the equality penalty parameter: it monotonically increases up to a predefined threshold and remains constant thereafter. Building upon this algorithmic framework, we establish comprehensive asymptotic complexities. We prove that Prox-PEP achieves an $\mathcal{O}(T^{-1/4})$ average expected oracle complexity for $ε$-KKT stationarity, specifically bounding the squared norm of the gradient of the Moreau envelope of the Lagrangian function, alongside constraint violations and complementarity conditions. Additionally, under standard light-tailed martingale noise assumptions, we derive an $\mathcal{O}(T^{-1/8})$ high-probability convergence bound for the norm of the gradient of the Lagrangian's Moreau envelope, as well as $\mathcal{O}(T^{-1/4})$ high-probability bounds for both constraint violations and complementarity conditions.

</details>


### [119] [Stability of Lagrangian Generalized Nash Equilibriums](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07173)
*Lixin Tang,Liwei Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了拉格朗日广义纳什均衡（LGNE）解集的稳定性性质，包括Aubin性质、孤立平静性和Lipschitz连续单值局部化，针对一般锥约束广义纳什均衡问题（GNEPs）给出了基于余导数和图导数的刻画，并特化到等式/不等式约束及共享锥约束等情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rockafellar (2024) 提出了拉格朗日广义纳什均衡（LGNE）的概念用于处理锥约束GNEPs，但该解集的稳定性性质（如Aubin性质、孤立平静性等）尚缺乏系统分析，需要建立理论刻画。

Method: 利用变分分析中的余导数（coderivative）和图导数（graph derivative）工具，对一般锥约束GNEPs的LGNE解映射在典型扰动下的稳定性进行刻画；进而特化到等式/不等式约束情形，通过线性互补系统的非奇异性给出显式刻画；最后分析共享锥约束和经典纳什均衡情形。

Result: 建立了一般锥约束GNEPs的LGNE解映射的Aubin性质和孤立平静性的余导数/图导数刻画；对于等式/不等式约束情形，给出了基于线性互补系统非奇异性的Lipschitz连续单值局部化与孤立平静性显式刻画；分析了共享锥约束下共识LGNE解映射的稳定性；给出了经典锥约束纳什均衡问题的LGNE解映射稳定性结果。

Conclusion: 本文为锥约束GNEPs的LGNE解集稳定性提供了完整的变分分析框架，所得刻画可用于进一步研究算法的收敛性分析及灵敏度分析。

Abstract: Lagrangian generalized Nash equilibriums (LGNEs) were introduced by Rockafellar (2024) for a class of generalized Nash equilibrium problems (GNEPs) in which each player's strategy is subject to conic constraints. This paper investigates the stability properties of the LGNE solution set, specifically focusing on the Aubin property, isolated calmness, and Lipschitz continuous single-valued localization. For general conically constrained GNEPs, characterizations of the Aubin property and isolated calmness of the LGNE solution mapping under canonical perturbations are established. These characterizations are formulated using the coderivative and graph derivative of normal cone mappings. Subsequently, these general results are specialized to GNEPs with equality and inequality constraints, yielding explicit characterizations for both the Lipschitz continuous single-valued localization and isolated calmness of the corresponding LGNE solution mapping, which are described by nonsingularity of linear complementarity sytems. For GNEPs with shared conic constraints, the Aubin property and isolated calmness of the consensus LGNE solution mapping--where identical Lagrange multipliers are assigned to the shared constraint--are first characterized. We further analyze the case when the conic constraints are specialized as equalities and inequalities. Finally, for classical conically constrained Nash equilibrium problems, the Aubin property and isolated calmness of the Lagrangian Nash equilibrium solution mapping are also analyzed.

</details>


### [120] [Dynamical Systems in Elliptical Pursuit and Evasion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07217)
*Sota Yoshihara*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究一对一追逃问题中圆形与椭圆形轨道的差异，基于Barton-Eliezer微分方程建立动力学系统。圆形轨道下系统自治且存在渐近稳定平衡点；椭圆轨道下系统非自治且无平衡点。通过复变量变换处理捕获奇异性，证明了快速追击者有限时间捕获并给出上界，慢速追击者系统存在唯一全局渐近稳定的周期解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究逃避者沿圆形与椭圆形轨道运动时，追逃问题动力学行为的本质差异，以及不同速度比下捕获与逃逸的临界条件。

Method: 基于Barton-Eliezer联立微分方程，假设追击者轨迹形状不受逃避者速度影响，建立关于速度矢量角差与分离距离的动力学系统。采用复变量变换（包含对数距离和角度差信息）处理捕获奇异性，分别对圆形和椭圆形逃避轨道进行自治/非自治系统分析。

Result: 圆形轨道：系统自治，存在渐近稳定平衡点。椭圆轨道：系统非自治，无平衡点。追击者更快时：有限时间捕获，给出显式捕获时间上界；追击者更慢时：系统存在唯一周期解，所有轨迹全局渐近收敛于此。

Conclusion: 逃避者轨道几何形状（圆形vs椭圆）从根本上改变了追逃系统的动力学性质（自治vs非自治、有无平衡点），并决定了不同速度比下的捕获/逃逸行为模式，为追逃问题的理论分析提供了新的数学框架。

Abstract: This paper investigates the difference between the circular and elliptical cases in one-on-one pursuit and evasion problems. Using the simultaneous differential equation derived by Barton and Eliezer, we derive a dynamical system based on the assumption that the shape of the pursuer's trajectory is unaffected by the evader's speed. The dynamical system involves the angular difference between the velocity vectors of the players and their separation distance. When the evader orbits a circle, the dynamical system is autonomous with an asymptotically stable equilibrium point. By contrast, if the evader orbits an ellipse, the dynamical system becomes non-autonomous and lacks an equilibrium point. To handle the singularity at capture, we reformulate the system using a complex variable that includes information about the logarithmic distance and the angular difference. We establish two main results: when the pursuer is faster than the evader, the pursuer captures the evader in finite time, and we derive an explicit upper bound for the capture time; when the pursuer is slower, the system possesses a unique periodic solution to which all trajectories converge globally and asymptotically.

</details>


### [121] [Data-Driven Contextual-Aware Uncertainty Set for Robust Dispatch of Power Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07441)
*Zhaojun Ruan,Yulin Liu,Le Fu,Libao Shi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于条件高斯混合模型的数据驱动方法，利用协变量侧信息构建上下文感知的不确定集，以应对电力系统鲁棒调度中不规则数据分布问题，并采用并集子集公式化和混合整数线性重构描述最坏情况，通过鲁棒机组组合数值实验验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有鲁棒优化中不确定集设计很少利用上下文侧信息，难以刻画协变量与不确定参数间的显式关系，尤其在不规则数据分布下，导致保守性和计算负担无法有效平衡。

Method: 基于条件高斯混合模型，利用协变量作为侧信息构建上下文感知不确定集；采用并集子集（union-of-subsets）公式化不确定集，并通过混合整数线性重构描述所有子集上的最坏情况实现。

Result: 通过鲁棒机组组合数值实验证明了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提方法能够利用侧信息构建适应不规则数据分布的上下文感知不确定集，为鲁棒调度提供了更灵活且计算可行的方案。

Abstract: Both the level of conservativeness and the computational burden in robust optimization are critically influenced by uncertainty set design. However, contextual side information is rarely exploited in robust dispatch of power systems characterized by irregular data distributions, which hinders the explicit characterization of the relationship between covariates and uncertain parameters. To address this issue, a data-driven method for constructing contextual-aware uncertainty set is proposed in this letter. Based on a conditional Gaussian mixture model, a set of covariates is leveraged as side information to design uncertainty sets tailored to historical data exhibiting irregular distributions. The resulting set is formulated as a union-of-subsets formulation, and a mixed integer linear reformulation is adopted to describe the worst-case realization across all subsets. Finally, the effectiveness of the proposed method is demonstrated through numerical experiments applied to robust unit commitment.

</details>


### [122] [Robust stochastic first order methods in heavy-tailed noise via medoid mini-batch gradient sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07634)
*Manojlo Vukovic,Dusan Jakovetic*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为R-SGD-Mini的鲁棒随机梯度下降方法，通过将大小为K的数据批次分成M个数据块，计算每个块的随机梯度，并选择这些梯度的medoid作为更新方向。该方法在对称重尾梯度噪声和非凸设置下，实现了期望时间平均平方梯度范数以O(T^{-1})速率收敛到零的小邻域，若已知时间范围则达到O(T^{-1/2})速率。引入裁剪后还能获得高概率收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于梯度裁剪或非线性算子的方法在处理重尾噪声（可能具有无限方差）时已有诸多变体，但作者希望探索一种替代路径，通过medoid mini-batch梯度采样的方式实现鲁棒优化，而无需依赖梯度裁剪等操作。

Method: R-SGD-Mini方法的核心步骤：(1) 将大小为K的数据批次拆分为M个独立数据块；(2) 对每个数据块计算随机梯度；(3) 从M个梯度中选取medoid（中位数方向）作为更新方向。此外还考虑了结合裁剪的变体版本。

Result: 在对称重尾梯度噪声和非凸设置下，期望时间平均平方梯度范数以O(T^{-1})速率收敛到零的小邻域（该邻域由噪声和算法参数显式刻画）；若已知时间范围则达到O(T^{-1/2})速率。引入裁剪后获得高概率收敛保证且保持相同速率。实验结果表明R-SGD-Mini及其裁剪变体在性能上一致优于SGD、裁剪SGD和基于均值中位数（Median-of-Means）的方法。

Conclusion: R-SGD-Mini通过medoid梯度采样的新颖方式，为处理重尾噪声的随机优化提供了一种有效替代方案，在理论和实验上均展现出竞争力。

Abstract: We consider a first order stochastic optimization framework where, at each iteration, $K$ independent identically distributed (i.i.d.) data point samples are drawn, based on which stochastic gradients can be queried. We allow gradient noise to be heavy-tailed, with possibly infinite variances. For the considered heavy-tailed setting, many algorithmic variants have recently been proposed based on gradient clipping or other nonlinear operators (e.g., normalization) applied over noisy gradients. In this paper, we take an alternative approach and propose a novel stochastic first order method dubbed Robust Stochastic Gradient Descent with medoid mini-batch gradient sampling, R-SGD-Mini for short. The core idea of R-SGD-Mini is to split the $K$-sized data batch into $M$ distinct data chunks, form for each chunk the stochastic gradient, and update the solution estimate with respect to the stochastic gradient direction of the chunk that is medoid of gradients of all data-chunks. Under a general class of symmetric heavy-tailed gradient noises and a standard non-convex setting, we establish explicit bounds on the expected time-averaged squared gradient norm. More precisely, we show that the latter quantity converges at rate $\mathcal{O}(T^{-1})$ to a small neighborhood of zero; we explicitly characterize this neighborhood in terms of noise and algorithm's parameters. Moreover, if the time horizon is known in advance, we establish the rate of $\mathcal{O}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}).$ Furthermore, when clipping is incorporated, we obtain convergence guaranties in the high-probability sense and recover the same rate. Experimental results indicate that R-SGD-Mini and its clipped variant consistently perform favorably compared to SGD, clipped SGD and Median-of-Means based methods.

</details>


### [123] [Scalable Distributed Stochastic Optimization via Bidirectional Compression: Beyond Pessimistic Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07795)
*Grigory Begunov,Alexander Tyurin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对分布式学习中随机梯度方法的通信-计算时间权衡问题，提出了Inkheart SGD和M4两种新的压缩方法，在必要且不限制问题类别的额外结构性假设下，打破了现有下界给出的悲观屏障，实现了随worker数量n扩展的新时间复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有下界表明在标准假设下，分布式随机梯度方法无法同时利用worker数量n来加速通信项(dκLΔ/ε)和随机梯度方差项(hσ²LΔ/ε²)，即使使用随机稀疏化技术。这导致非分布式SGD或同步SGD已能达到最优，压缩方法的进展似乎无效。本文旨在挑战这一限制。

Method: 提出了两种新的压缩方法Inkheart SGD和M4，在额外结构性假设（该假设是必要的且不限制问题类别）下，设计新的压缩通信策略，使得算法复杂度能随worker数量n扩展。

Result: 新方法实现了新的最坏情况时间复杂度，打破了此前下界给出的悲观屏障，允许通信项和方差项同时随n改善。

Conclusion: 通过引入合理的结构性假设，压缩方法可以超越经典下界，实现随worker数量扩展的有效加速。

Abstract: In centralized, distributed, and federated learning with stochastic gradients and $n$ workers, it was recently shown that it is infeasible to find an $\varepsilon$-stationary point faster than $\tildeΩ\left(\min\left\{\frac{d κL Δ}{\varepsilon} + \frac{h L Δ}{\varepsilon} + \frac{h σ^2 L Δ}{n \varepsilon^2},\; \frac{h σ^2 L Δ}{\varepsilon^2} + \frac{h L Δ}{\varepsilon}\right\}\right)$ seconds in both homogeneous and heterogeneous settings under standard assumptions: $L$-smoothness, $σ^2$-bounded unbiased stochastic gradients, and lower boundedness of the function, i.e., $f(x) \geq f^*$ for all $x \in \mathbb{R}^d$, where $Δ= f(x^0) - f^*$, $h$ is the computation time, $κ$ is the communication speed between the workers and the server, and $d$ is the dimension of the iterates and gradients. This result is pessimistic since it does not allow a complexity in which both $\frac{d κL Δ}{\varepsilon}$ and $\frac{h σ^2 L Δ}{\varepsilon^2}$ improve with $n$, even when using random sparsification techniques; moreover, this lower bound can be matched by either non-distributed SGD or vanilla Synchronous SGD, which reduces the impact of recent progress in the design of compression-based methods. In this work, we challenge this limitation and propose new compressed methods, Inkheart SGD and M4, and show that under an additional structural assumption, which is necessary due to the lower bound and which does not restrict the class of considered problems, we achieve new state-of-the-art time complexities that break this pessimistic barrier and allow scaling with the number of workers $n$.

</details>


### [124] [Robust Capacity Expansion under Wildfire Ignition Risk and High Renewable Penetration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07880)
*Tomás Tapia,Ryan Piansky,Yury Dvorkin,Jean-Paul Watson*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个鲁棒优化模型，用于确定电池储能系统选址和输电线路地下化投资，以应对由 wildfire 风险导致的线路停运和可再生能源波动。模型采用混合整数线性规划（MILP）和列与约束生成算法求解，在圣地亚哥电力系统上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来电力系统引发野火的风险显著增加，运营商常通过停运输电线路来降低风险。随着可变可再生能源并网增加，在野火风险下管理和准备系统停运变得更具挑战性。因此需要投资电池储能和线路地下化等缓解措施，以减少停运和可再生能源波动带来的风险及不利影响。

Method: 构建鲁棒优化模型，利用代表性周和不确定性集捕捉不确定变量的时间关系；考虑点火风险导致线路停运的最坏情况与可再生能源可用性的最坏情况；模型为MILP问题，通过对偶理论和二元分解处理非线性，使用列与约束生成算法求解。

Result: 在圣地亚哥电力系统模型上进行了评估，证明了该方法在提升系统对野火风险的韧性方面具有实际有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的投资决策框架能够有效应对野火风险下的系统不确定性，提升电力系统韧性。

Abstract: In power systems, the risk of wildfire ignition has increased significantly in recent years. The impact and severity of these events on energy dispatch, as well as their societal ramifications, make wildfire prevention critical for power system planning and operation. A common intervention by system operators is to de-energize transmission lines to mitigate the risk of fire caused by equipment failures. With the growing integration of variable renewable generation, managing and preparing the system to de-energization under wildfire risk has become even more challenging. In this context, mitigation decisions such as installing battery energy storage systems and undergrounding transmission lines can reduce the risk and adverse effects associated with de-energization and renewable generation variability. This paper presents a robust optimization model to determine the optimal location of battery storage and undergrounding of transmission line investment, utilizing representative weeks and uncertainty sets to capture the temporal relationship of uncertain variables. Specifically, this paper addresses: (i) the worst-case realization of ignition risk leading to the de-energization of transmission lines, combined with the worst-case realization of renewable energy availability, and (ii) the optimal investment decisions for energy storage capacity and undergrounding of transmission lines that are exposed to ignition risk. The proposed model is formulated as a mixed-integer linear programming (MILP) problem, employing duality theory and binary decomposition to address nonlinearities, and is solved using a column-and-constraint generation algorithm. The proposed framework is evaluated on a model of the San Diego power system, demonstrating its practical effectiveness in improving the resilience to wildfire risk.

</details>


### [125] [Penalty-Based First-Order Methods for Bilevel Optimization with Minimax and Constrained Lower-Level Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08006)
*Yiyang Shen,Yutian He,Weiran Wang,Qihang Lin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究上下层均为极小极大形式的双层优化问题，提出基于惩罚的一阶方法，无需下层强凸性假设。确定条件下达到$\tilde{O}(ε^{-4})$复杂度，并将带凸约束的下层极小化问题通过拉格朗日对偶转化为该框架，改进复杂度从$\tilde{O}(ε^{-7})$到$\tilde{O}(ε^{-4})$。随机条件下达到$\tilde{O}(ε^{-9})$复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双层优化方法主要处理下层为极小化问题且常需强凸性假设，而许多新兴应用中下层为极小极大结构，现有方法无法直接适用。本文旨在填补这一空白，开发无需下层强凸性的双层极小极大优化方法。

Method: 提出基于惩罚的一阶方法，将双层极小极大优化转化为可处理的单层优化问题。确定条件下证明方法可找到$ε$-KKT点，并利用拉格朗日对偶将带凸约束的下层极小化问题转化为框架特例。进一步扩展到随机梯度情形。

Result: 确定条件下：$\tilde{O}(ε^{-4})$ oracle复杂度找到$ε$-KKT点；凸约束下层极小化问题：复杂度从$\tilde{O}(ε^{-7})$改进至$\tilde{O}(ε^{-4})$；随机条件下：$\tilde{O}(ε^{-9})$复杂度找到近似$ε$-KKT点。

Conclusion: 本文成功解决了下层为极小极大结构的双层优化问题，提出的惩罚方法在理论和复杂度上具有优势，尤其在对偶转化中显著改进了现有结果，为更广泛的双层优化应用提供了理论保障。

Abstract: We study a class of bilevel optimization problems in which both the upper- and lower-level problems have minimax structures. This setting captures a broad range of emerging applications. Despite the extensive literature on bilevel optimization and minimax optimization separately, existing methods mainly focus on bilevel optimization with lower-level minimization problems, often under strong convexity assumptions, and are not directly applicable to the minimax lower-level setting considered here. To address this gap, we develop penalty-based first-order methods for bilevel minimax optimization without requiring strong convexity of the lower-level problem. In the deterministic setting, we establish that the proposed method finds an $ε$-KKT point with $\tilde{O}(ε^{-4})$ oracle complexity. We further show that bilevel problems with convex constrained lower-level minimization can be reformulated as special cases of our framework via Lagrangian duality, leading to an $\tilde{O}(ε^{-4})$ complexity bound that improves upon the existing $\tilde{O}(ε^{-7})$ result. Finally, we extend our approach to the stochastic setting, where only stochastic gradient oracles are available, and prove that the proposed stochastic method finds a nearly $ε$-KKT point with $\tilde{O}(ε^{-9})$ oracle complexity.

</details>


### [126] [Approximate directional stationarity and associated qualification conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08026)
*Isabella Käming,Patrick Mehlitz*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文融合了近似平稳性与方向平稳性两个概念，提出了"近似方向平稳性"（approximate directional stationarity）这一新的最优性条件，并针对带有非光滑几何约束的优化问题进行了深入研究。同时，提出了一个基于Mangasarian-Fromovitz类型的充分性条件，使得从近似方向平稳点可以推断出方向平稳性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近似平稳性条件通过扰动系统的解来提供必要最优性条件，无需额外假设；方向平稳性条件则通过纳入临界方向数据来锐化标准平稳性条件。本文旨在融合这两个概念，研究近似方向平稳性，并建立从近似方向平稳性推断方向平稳性的资格条件。

Method: 以带有非光滑几何约束的优化问题为模型，定义并研究近似方向平稳性；提出一种基于Mangasarian-Fromovitz类型的资格条件，该条件依赖于验证近似方向平稳性的特定序列，仅需使用极限变分分析中的方向性工具检查一个简单条件。

Result: 详细研究了近似方向平稳性作为必要最优性条件的作用，补充了文献中的结果；提出了一个资格条件，可以从一个近似方向平稳点推断其方向平稳性，该条件不同于需要所有序列稳定行为的标准近似约束资格。

Conclusion: 建立了多种验证局部极小点方向平稳性的途径，并通过示例使理论结果更易理解。所提出的资格条件仅需检查特定序列，比标准近似约束资格条件更易验证。

Abstract: Approximate stationarity conditions provide necessary optimality conditions without requiring additional assumptions by demanding that a perturbed stationarity system possesses solutions as the involved perturbations tend to zero. Together with associated approximate constraint qualifications, which are typically rather mild, they raised much interest in the optimization community during the last decade. In parallel, directional stationarity conditions became quite popular as they sharpen standard stationarity conditions by incorporating data associated with underlying critical directions. The purpose of this paper is twofold. First, we melt the aforementioned concepts of approximate and directional stationarity to formulate and study so-called approximate directional stationarity. For the underlying model problem, an optimization problem with nonsmooth geometric constraints is chosen, which covers diverse practically relevant applications. The role of approximate directional stationarity as a necessary optimality condition is investigated in much detail, complementing results from the literature. Second, we formulate a qualification condition which, based on an approximately directionally stationary point, can be exploited to infer its directional stationarity. The latter condition depends on one particular sequence verifying approximate directional stationarity and merely requires to check a simple condition of Mangasarian--Fromovitz type stated in terms of the directional tools of limiting variational analysis. This contrasts standard approximate constraint qualifications that typically demand a certain stable behavior of all sequences validating approximate stationarity. Throughout, various approaches to verify directional stationarity of local minimizers are established, and illustrative examples are presented to make the theoretical results more accessible.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [127] [RateQuant: Optimal Mixed-Precision KV Cache Quantization via Rate-Distortion Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06675)
*Fei Zuo,Zikang Zhou,Hao Cong,Xiaoyan Xi,Ho Fai Leung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RateQuant方法，通过为每个量化器拟合独立的失真模型D(b)=α·β^{-b}，解决混合精度KV cache量化中的失真模型不匹配问题，利用逆向注水算法进行闭式比特分配，在Qwen3-8B上以2.5平均比特将KIVI的困惑度从49.3降至14.9（降幅70%），并提升QuaRot 6.6 PPL，校准仅需1.6秒且无推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV cache量化方法对所有注意力头分配相同比特数，忽略了不同头的重要性差异。混合精度分配虽直观合理，但存在隐蔽缺陷：不同量化器具有不同的失真曲线D(b)=α·β^{-b}，β在3.6~5.3之间变化，将一个量化器的失真模型应用于另一个会导致分配顺序反转，性能甚至不如均匀量化，即失真模型不匹配问题。

Method: 1) 从小型校准集为每个量化器拟合独立的失真模型D(b)=α·β^{-b}；2) 基于率失真理论，通过逆向注水算法（reverse waterfilling）求解比特分配问题的闭式解，以消除量化器间失真模型差异带来的分配错误。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B上以2.5平均比特：将KIVI的困惑度从49.3降至14.9（相对降低70%）；提升QuaRot 6.6 PPL；整个校准过程仅需1.6秒（单GPU），推理阶段零开销。

Conclusion: 失真模型不匹配是混合精度KV cache量化的关键失败模式，RateQuant通过逐量化器建模与率失真优化有效解决了该问题，实现了显著优于均匀量化的性能且几乎无额外代价。

Abstract: Large language models cache all previously computed key-value (KV) pairs during generation, and this KV cache grows linearly with sequence length, making it a primary memory bottleneck for serving. Quantizing the KV cache to fewer bits reduces this cost, yet all current quantizers assign the same bit-width to every attention head, ignoring the large variation in head importance. A natural idea is to allocate more bits to important heads and fewer to the rest. We show, however, that such mixed-precision allocation has a hidden pitfall: each quantizer follows a different distortion curve D(b)=alpha*beta^{-b}, and the decay rate beta varies from 3.6 to 5.3 across quantizer designs. Applying one quantizer's distortion model to another inverts the allocation order and makes performance worse than uniform quantization. We call this failure mode distortion model mismatch and propose RateQuant to resolve it. RateQuant fits a per-quantizer distortion model from a small calibration set, then solves the resulting bit-allocation problem in closed form via reverse waterfilling from rate-distortion theory. On Qwen3-8B at 2.5 average bits, calibrated RateQuant reduces KIVI's perplexity from 49.3 to 14.9 (70% reduction) and improves QuaRot by 6.6 PPL. The entire calibration takes 1.6 s on a single GPU and adds zero overhead at inference time.

</details>


### [128] [Breaking the Illusion: When Positive Meets Negative in Multimodal Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06679)
*Yubo Jiang,Yitong An,Xin Yang,Abudukelimu Wuerkaixi,Xuxin Cheng,Fengying Xie,Zhiguo Jiang,Cao Liu,Ke Zeng,Haopeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PND（正负解码）框架，通过对比正路径（增强视觉证据）和负路径（惩罚先验主导生成）的解码输出，无需训练即可减少VLM中的物体幻觉问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLMs因过度依赖语言先验而频繁产生物体幻觉（生成与视觉现实矛盾的内容），现有方法需要重训练或微调，成本高且不灵活。

Method: 提出PND框架，包含双路径对比：正路径放大视觉证据，负路径构建反事实以惩罚先验主导生成；在解码阶段对比两条路径的输出，引导生成朝向视觉对齐的结果。

Result: 在POPE、MME、CHAIR基准上达到最先进性能，且无需重新训练模型。

Conclusion: PND通过无训练的推理干预有效缓解物体幻觉，验证了VLM中注意力不平衡（视觉特征权重不足）是导致幻觉的关键因素。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are frequently undermined by object hallucination, generating content that contradicts visual reality, due to an over-reliance on linguistic priors. We introduce Positive-and-Negative Decoding (PND), a training-free inference framework that intervenes directly in the decoding process to enforce visual fidelity. PND is motivated by our finding of an attention imbalance in VLMs, where visual features are under-weighted. Our framework introduces a dual-path contrast: a positive path that amplifies visual evidence and a negative path that constructs counterfactuals to penalize prior-dominant generation. By contrasting outputs from both paths during decoding, PND steers generation toward visually grounded results. Experiments on POPE, MME, and CHAIR demonstrate state-of-the-art performance without retraining.

</details>


### [129] [On the Role of Strain and Vorticity in Numerical Integration Error for Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06680)
*Chenxi Tao,Seung-Kyum Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了流匹配生成模型中速度场的应变率和涡度对积分误差的不同影响，证明应变通过对数范数导致指数级误差放大，而涡度仅贡献线性截断误差。最优传输速度场具有无旋和零物质导数特性，可实现二阶欧拉精度。基于此提出加权雅可比正则化方法，在NFE=5时误差降低2.7倍，CIFAR-10上NFE=10时FID提升14%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配模型的推理成本由积分步数(NFE)直接决定，理解速度场中哪些性质控制积分误差，从而设计更高效的生成模型。

Method: 将速度场的雅可比矩阵分解为对称部分S（应变率）和反对称部分Ω（涡度），理论分析二者对积分误差的不同贡献；提出加权雅可比正则化方法（应变权重α、涡度权重β）。

Result: 二维合成数据上NFE=5时积分误差降低2.7倍；CIFAR-10上轻量微调后NFE=10时FID提升14%，同时保持高NFE下的生成质量。

Conclusion: 应变率主导误差放大（指数级），涡度仅贡献线性误差；最优传输速度场具有无旋性，可实现二阶精度；提出的正则化方法能有效降低低NFE下的积分误差。

Abstract: Flow matching generates data by integrating a learned velocity field, where the number of integration steps (NFE) directly determines inference cost. We analyze which properties of the velocity field govern integration error by decomposing the velocity Jacobian into its symmetric part S (strain rate) and antisymmetric part Omega (vorticity). We prove that strain and vorticity play different roles: strain controls exponential error amplification through the logarithmic norm, while vorticity contributes only linearly to the local truncation error. We further show that the optimal transport velocity field is irrotational and has zero material derivative, implying second-order Euler accuracy; for exact displacement interpolation, the associated Lagrangian particle dynamics are integrated exactly by Euler. Motivated by this analysis, we study weighted Jacobian regularization with strain weight alpha and vorticity weight beta. Experiments on 2D synthetic data confirm the main theoretical predictions, showing up to 2.7x lower integration error at NFE=5. Preliminary CIFAR-10 experiments show consistent trends, with a lightweight fine-tuning procedure improving FID by 14 percent at NFE=10 while preserving high-NFE quality.

</details>


### [130] [A Hierarchical Ensemble Pipeline for Anomaly Detection in ESA Satellite Telemetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06681)
*Lorenzo Riccardo Allegrini,Geremia Pompei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种层次化集成流水线，结合形状基元与统计特征提取、逐通道建模、通道内堆叠及跨通道聚合，用于多变量遥测数据异常检测，在ESA-ADB基准上表现出强泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决欧洲航天局(ESA)多变量遥测数据中的异常检测问题，传统方法难以捕捉卫星数据中细微、复杂的异常模式。

Method: 层次化集成流水线：形状基元与统计特征提取 → 逐通道建模 → 通道内堆叠(Intra-channel Stacking) → 跨通道聚合(Cross-channel Aggregation)；使用时序交叉验证和两级掩码策略防止信息泄露。

Result: 在ESA-ADB基准挑战上表现出强泛化能力，有效检测真实卫星遥测中的细微异常。

Conclusion: 层次化建模方法在检测细微异常方面具有有效性，为卫星遥测异常检测提供了可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: A hierarchical ensemble pipeline is introduced to address anomaly detection in multivariate telemetry data provided by European Space Agency (ESA). The method integrates shapelet-based and statistical feature extraction, per-channel modeling, intra-channel stacking, and a final cross-channel aggregation. The pipeline is trained and validated using time-series cross-validation and two-level masking strategies to prevent information leakage. Results on the European Space Agency Anomaly Detection Benchmark (ESA-ADB) challenge demonstrate strong generalization, highlighting the effectiveness of hierarchical modeling in detecting subtle anomalies in realistic satellite telemetry.

</details>


### [131] [Toeplitz MLP Mixers are Low Complexity, Information-Rich Sequence Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06683)
*Benjamin L. Badger,Ethan Roland*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Toeplitz MLP Mixer（TMM），一种替代Transformer的架构，用三角掩蔽Toeplitz矩阵乘法替代注意力机制，实现O(dn log n)训练时间和O(dn)推理复杂度，在信息保留和上下文学习方面优于可比架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer自注意力机制具有二次时间和空间复杂度，限制了大规模语言模型的效率。作者希望设计一种次二次复杂度的架构，同时保持或提升模型性能。

Method: 提出Toeplitz MLP Mixer (TMM)，用三角掩蔽Toeplitz矩阵乘法替代注意力机制，在序列维度上进行高效矩阵乘法，实现次二次训练和线性推理复杂度。

Result: TMM在训练效率和设备内存方面优于其他次二次架构；具有更好的信息保留能力和复制能力；在信息检索和上下文学习基准上表现更优。从算子指标理论分析发现，训练后的因果非可逆模型的Toeplitz层更可能是可逆或接近可逆的。

Conclusion: TMM通过简单的Toeplitz矩阵乘法实现高效计算，得益于缺乏架构偏见而保留更多输入信息，在多项任务上优于可比架构。此外，算子指标理论揭示了可逆性与模型性能之间的反直觉关系。

Abstract: Transformer-based large language models are in some respects limited by the quadratic time and space computational complexity of attention. We introduce the Toeplitz MLP Mixer (TMM), a transformer-like architecture that swaps attention for triangular-masked Toeplitz matrix multiplication over the sequence dimension resulting in $\mathcal{O} (dn \log n)$ time and $\mathcal O(dn)$ space complexity during training and $\mathcal O(dn)$ time and space at inference prefill. Despite the lack of sophisticated input modulation or state maintenance present in other sub-quadratic architectures, TMMs yield greater training efficiency in terms of loss achieved per compute and device memory. We demonstrate that TMMs are capable of retaining more input information resulting in improved copying ability, which we argue results from a lack of architectural biases. Consistent with higher input information retention, TMMs exhibit superior information retrieval and in-context learning benchmark accuracy compared to comparable architectures. We conclude with an analysis from the perspective of operator index theory and show that, counterintuitively, trained Toeplitz layers of causal non-invertible models are more likely to be invertible or nearly so than models that are actually invertible over their inputs.

</details>


### [132] [From Canopy to Collision: A Hybrid Predictive Framework for Identifying Risk Factors in Tree-Involved Traffic Crashes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06684)
*Abdul Azim,Ahmed Hossain,Soumyadip Maitra,Panick Kalambay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用CRSS数据库（2020-2023）构建了包含CatBoost分类、SHAP解释、逻辑回归验证和交互效应分析的多步骤框架，识别出树木碰撞事故严重性的关键风险因素。结果显示：未使用安全带是最强预测因子（严重伤亡风险增加近3倍），车辆年龄、超速和驾驶员损伤也具有显著影响，并发现光照与车龄、超速与光照等关键交互效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 树木碰撞是路外碰撞中致死率极高的一类事故，但以往研究缺乏对多因素交互效应的系统量化分析。本研究旨在建立一个综合框架，不仅识别风险因素，还要量化其边际效应及交互作用，为安全系统干预提供数据驱动的决策依据。

Method: 采用四步分析框架：(1) CatBoost机器学习模型识别影响事故严重性二元分类（KA级 vs BC级）的关键因素；(2) SHAP工具量化并可视化各因素的边际效应；(3) 二元Logistic回归模型估计因子效应并验证SHAP重要性；(4) SHAP交互图分析关键因素的联合效应。

Result: 未使用安全带是最强预测因子（未系安全带者严重伤亡概率高出近3倍），车辆年龄、超速违规和驾驶员损伤也显著影响严重性。发现光照条件与车龄、超速与光照条件、安全带使用与车龄、路面状况与超速之间存在关键交互效应，表现出风险叠加特性。

Conclusion: 研究为安全系统干预提供了针对性建议：加强安全带执法、在能见度降低条件下进行速度管理、推进车辆现代化更新。揭示了多因素交互效应对事故严重性的叠加影响，超越了单因素分析的局限性。

Abstract: Tree-involved crashes represent a critical subset of run-off-road (ROR) collisions, often resulting in fatal or severe injuries due to high-energy impacts. This study develops a comprehensive analytical framework to identify and quantify risk factors contributing to crash severity in tree-involved collisions using the Crash Report Sampling System (CRSS) database spanning 2020-2023. The modeling framework follows a multi-step process. First, a machine learning based classification model (CatBoost) identifies key factors associated with binary crash injury severity (KA: fatal or incapacitating injury versus BC: non-incapacitating or possible injury). Second, SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP) tool is used to quantify and visualize the marginal effects of top influential factors on crash severity. Third, a binary logistic regression model estimates factor effects and validates SHAP-derived importance measures. Finally, SHAP interaction plots examine the combined effects of key contributing factors. Results reveal restraint non-use as the most influential predictor, with unrestrained occupants nearly three times more likely to experience severe outcomes due to ejection risk. Vehicle age, speeding violations, and driver impairment demonstrate substantial effects, reflecting reduced crashworthiness, increased impact forces, and reduced control capabilities. Critical interactions emerge between lighting conditions and vehicle age, speeding and lighting conditions, restraint use and vehicle age, and road surface and speeding, demonstrating additive risk effects with specific interactions. These findings provide critical insights for targeted safe system-based interventions, including enhanced seat belt enforcement, speed management in reduced visibility conditions, and vehicle fleet modernization.

</details>


### [133] [Robustness of Refugee-Matching Gains to Off-Policy Evaluation Choices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06686)
*Kirk Bansak,Elisabeth Paulson,Dominik Rothenhäusler,Jeremy Ferwerda,Jens Hainmueller,Michael Hotard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在Bansak等人(2018)研究基础上，使用多种离策略评估方法（包括IPW和多种AIPW变体）验证了美国难民匹配场景下反事实影响评估结果的稳定性，发现估计值在所有场景中量级一致且多数统计显著，并与原始结果一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证难民匹配策略的反事实影响评估结果在不同评估方法下的稳健性，确保此前研究发现（Bansak et al., 2018）并非特定方法或建模选择下的偶然结果。

Method: 采用多种离策略评估方法：逆概率加权（IPW）和增强逆概率加权（AIPW）的多个变体；同时考虑替代建模架构和不同分配程序等修改方案进行对比分析。

Result: 所有场景下影响估计值的量级保持一致，大多数情况下统计显著，且与Bansak等人(2018)的原始结果一致。

Conclusion: 美国难民匹配的反事实影响评估结果在不同评估方法、建模架构和分配程序下具有高度稳健性。

Abstract: Previous research has investigated the potential of refugee matching for boosting refugee outcomes, first considered by Bansak et al. (2018). This paper demonstrates the stability of counterfactual impact evaluation results in the context of refugee matching in the United States using a range of off-policy evaluation methods. In order to estimate counterfactual impact and test the robustness of our results, we employ several evaluation methods, including inverse probability weighting (IPW) and multiple variants of augmented inverse probability weighting (AIPW). We also consider various modifications, including alternative modeling architectures and different assignment procedures. The impact estimates remain consistent in magnitude in all scenarios as well as statistically significant in most cases. Furthermore, the estimates are also consistent with the results originally presented in Bansak et al. (2018).

</details>


### [134] [Conditional generation of antibody sequences with classifier-guided germline-absorbing discrete diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06720)
*Justin Sanders,Luca Giancardo,Lan Guo,Yue Zhao,Kemal Sonmez,Nina Cheng,Melih Yilmaz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出胚系吸收扩散（germline absorbing diffusion）方法，通过将胚系序列设为吸收态改进离散扩散微调，使抗体非胚系残基预测准确率从26%提升至46%，并在条件生成任务（疏水性和结合亲和度）上优于EvoProtGrad。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有抗体设计方法存在两个关键局限：一是模型主要记忆胚系序列而非学习有生物学意义的体细胞变异；二是对基于分类器的条件生成支持有限。

Method: 提出胚系吸收扩散（germline absorbing diffusion），修改离散扩散噪声过程，将胚系序列（而非掩码序列）设为吸收态，使模型仅学习从胚系到观测序列的轨迹；结合离散扩散微调实现任意分类器条件生成。

Result: 非胚系残基预测准确率从26%提升至46%，接近理论上限；在改善疏水性和结合亲和度的条件生成任务中，类别遵循度与样本质量的权衡显著优于EvoProtGrad。

Conclusion: 胚系扩散通过生物学启发的归纳偏置有效缓解胚系偏差，提升了抗体序列建模和条件生成的质量。

Abstract: Antibody therapeutics are among the most successful modern medicines, yet computationally designing antibodies with desirable binding and developability properties remains challenging. While protein language models (pLMs) have emerged as powerful tools for antibody sequence design, existing approaches largely suffer from two key limitations: they predominantly memorize germline sequences rather than modeling biologically meaningful somatic variation, and they offer limited support for flexible classifier-guided conditional generation. We address these challenges through two primary contributions. First, we demonstrate that discrete diffusion fine-tuning achieves strong language modeling performance on antibody sequences while allowing for generation conditioned on any off-the-shelf classifier. Second, we introduce germline absorbing diffusion, a novel modification of the discrete diffusion noise process in which the germline sequence - rather than a masked sequence - serves as the absorbing state. This biologically motivated inductive bias restricts the model to learning the trajectory from germline to observed sequence, effectively excluding genetic variation and V(D)J recombination statistics from the learned distribution and dramatically mitigating germline bias. We show that germline diffusion improves non-germline residue prediction accuracy from 26 percent to 46 percent, approaching the theoretical upper bound set by true biological variability. We then demonstrate the utility of our germline diffusion model on the conditional generation tasks of sampling antibodies with improved hydrophobicity and predicted binding affinity. On both tasks our model shows an improved tradeoff between class adherence and sample quality, significantly outperforming EvoProtGrad, a popular strategy to sample from pLMs with gradient-based discrete Markov Chain Monte Carlo.

</details>


### [135] [Enabling Unsupervised Training of Deep EEG Denoisers With Intelligent Partitioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06724)
*Qiyu Rao,Haozhe Tian,Homayoun Hamedmoghadam,Danilo Mandic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为iPSD的自监督去噪方法，通过将EEG片段智能划分为独立的噪声实现来进行自监督学习，无需干净参考信号，在可穿戴EEG去噪中达到SOTA性能，尤其适用于低信噪比和强伪影场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统信号处理方法依赖固定/启发式规则，无法处理可穿戴EEG中时变的 pervasive 伪影；深度学习方法虽能进行无分解去噪，但训练需要无法获得的干净EEG作为参考。

Method: 提出Intelligent Partitioning for Self-supervised Denoising (iPSD)，通过将输入EEG片段智能划分为具有相同底层信号但独立噪声实现的子段，实现自监督深度学习去噪器的训练，支持零样本设置。

Result: 在可穿戴EEG（包括耳内传感器）上验证，iPSD达到SOTA性能，尤其在极低信噪比（低至-10 dB）和挑战性伪影（如EMG）下表现突出，频谱保真度比基线方法高出数个数量级。

Conclusion: iPSD通过自监督分区策略有效解决了可穿戴EEG去噪中缺乏干净参考信号的核心难题，为实际可穿戴脑电应用提供了可行的去噪方案。

Abstract: Denoising wearable electroencephalogram (EEG) is inherently challenging since neural activity is not only subtle but also inseparable from spectrally overlapping noise artifacts. Classical signal processing methods, relying on fixed or heuristic rules, cannot handle the time-varying pervasive artifacts in wearable EEGs. Deep learning methods, on the other hand, show promise in decomposition-free EEG denoising using highly expressive neural networks, but the training requires artifact-free EEG, which is inherently unobtainable. To address this, we propose Intelligent Partitioning for Self-supervised Denoising (iPSD). Our method eliminates the need for clean references by learning to partition an input EEG segment into independent noisy realizations with the same underlying signal. This enables self-supervision of deep learning denoisers, even in zero-shot settings where only a single EEG segment to be denoised is available. We validate iPSD through extensive experiments, including validations on wearable EEG from in-ear sensors. The results show that iPSD achieves state-of-the-art performance, most notably under extremely low signal-to-noise ratios (down to -10 dB) and challenging artifacts (e.g., EMG), with spectral fidelity orders of magnitude higher than competitive baselines.

</details>


### [136] [The E$Δ$-MHC-Geo Transformer: Adaptive Geodesic Operations with Guaranteed Orthogonality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06729)
*Arash Shahmansoori*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EΔ-MHC-Geo Transformer架构，通过数据依赖的Cayley旋转实现无条件正交残差连接，并引入混合门控机制处理Householder反射，在长期稳定性、近π旋转损失和范数保持上显著优于基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DDL的Householder算子仅在特定β值下保持正交性，且Cayley变换无法处理特征值-1（即反射/取反情形），导致无法访问O(n)的全部连通分量，限制了模型表达能力。

Method: 提出数据依赖的Cayley旋转Q(x)=(I+(β/2)A(x))^{-1}(I-(β/2)A(x))，对所有β和输入保持正交性；引入混合门控机制，通过可学习的算子选择门γ(X)将Cayley旋转与Householder反射H₂(X)结合；并设计中点坍缩正则化器4γ(1-γ)鼓励边界门决策。

Result: 在约1.79M参数/模型、3个种子的均值和标准差下，EΔ-MHC-Geo在长期稳定性上分别达到JPmHC的1.9倍和GPT的3.8倍；近π旋转损失在单平面上优于JPmHC 4.5倍；范数保持均偏差0.001；反射探针测试中余弦对齐达0.96，且层数减少33%。

Conclusion: JPmHC因有限Cayley残差混合器无法精确实现λ=-1算子且缺乏反射分支，本工作的混合方法通过访问O(n)的两个连通分量实现了更优的正交残差连接性能。

Abstract: We present the E$Δ$-MHC-Geo Transformer, a novel architecture that unifies Manifold-Constrained Hyper-Connections (mHC), Deep Delta Learning (DDL), and the Cayley transform to obtain input-adaptive, unconditionally orthogonal residual connections. Unlike DDL, whose Householder operator is orthogonal only at $β\in \{0,2\}$, our Data-Dependent Cayley rotation $Q(x)=(I+(β/2)A(x))^{-1}(I-(β/2)A(x))$ preserves orthogonality for all $β$ and all inputs. To handle negation, an eigenvalue $-1$ case that Cayley provably excludes, we introduce the E$Δ$-MHC-Geo Hybrid, which combines Cayley rotation with Householder reflection via a learned operator-selection gate $X'=γ(X)Q(X)X+(1-γ(X))H_2(X)X$. A midpoint-collapse regularizer, $4γ(1-γ)$, encourages boundary gate decisions, where each selected component is orthogonal. In matched-parameter comparisons, with approximately 1.79M parameters per model and mean +/- standard deviation over 3 seeds, against four baselines including the concurrent JPmHC, E$Δ$-MHC-Geo achieves the best long-horizon stability, 1.9x over JPmHC and 3.8x over GPT; the best near-$π$ rotation loss, 4.5x over JPmHC on single-plane; strong norm preservation, with 0.001 mean deviation; and 0.96 negation cosine alignment in a diagnostic reflection probe, all with 33% fewer layers. While JPmHC's wider representation excels on pure rotation, its finite Cayley residual mixer excludes an exact $λ=-1$ operator and has no reflection branch, motivating our hybrid approach for accessing both connected components of $O(n)$.

</details>


### [137] [Semantic State Abstraction Interfaces for LLM-Augmented Portfolio Decisions: Multi-Axis News Decomposition and RL Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06730)
*Likhita Yerra,Remi Uttejitha Allam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出语义状态抽象接口(SSAI)，一种将稀疏非结构化文本映射到K个可审计命名坐标的方法模板，用于分离顺序决策系统中的表征假设与优化方差。在美股数据上实例化K=4轴（情感、风险、信心、波动率预测），评估显示因子组合累积收益307.2%（Sharpe 1.067），但未能通过覆盖分层控制检验，在高成本下表现逆转，统计上弱于纯情感基线。定位SSAI为一种可解释性-性能诊断工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有顺序决策系统难以区分性能提升来自更好的状态表征还是更优的优化算法，尤其当输入为稀疏非结构化文本时，缺乏标准化的可审计接口来分离这两种效应。

Method: 设计SSAI模板：将非结构化文本映射到K个命名的可审计坐标轴（含无新闻日的默认中性值）。实例化K=4（情感、风险、信心、波动率预测），在30只纳斯达克100股票、FNSPID新闻数据（2019-2023）上，通过直接因子组合、监督岭回归预测器和共享固定φ的RL智能体（DP-PPO、SAC）进行评估。

Result: 四因子组合累积回报307.2%（买入持有243.6%），Sharpe 1.067；但收益在覆盖分层控制下不显著，在≥0.2%交易成本下逆转，统计上弱于纯情感基线。PC1主成分组合和FinBERT组合在该设定下表现出更强的排序信号。

Conclusion: SSAI作为可解释性-性能诊断工具和可复用协议，比声称其性能优于密集替代方案更有价值，适用于稀疏文本决策系统的表征与优化效应分离分析。

Abstract: We introduce Semantic State Abstraction Interfaces (SSAI): a methodological template for mapping sparse unstructured text into $K$ auditable, named coordinates with neutral defaults on no-news days, designed to separate representation hypotheses from optimisation variance in sequential decision systems. Our contribution is the framework and its evaluation protocol, not a claim that SSAI outperforms denser alternatives.
  We instantiate SSAI with $K=4$ axes (sentiment, risk, confidence, volatility forecast) on a US-equity panel (30 NASDAQ-100 names, FNSPID news, 2019--2023 test), and evaluate it across direct factor portfolios, supervised ridge forecasters, and RL agents (DP-PPO, SAC) that share the same fixed $φ$. The four-factor factor portfolio reaches 307.2% cumulative return and Sharpe 1.067, but apparent gains versus buy-and-hold (243.6%) fail coverage-stratified controls, reverse at $\geq 0.2$% costs, and are statistically fragile versus a sentiment-only baseline; a PC1 composite and a FinBERT portfolio baseline are stronger ranking signals in this setting. Ridge and RL blocks diagnose representation versus optimiser effects. We position SSAI as an interpretability-performance diagnostic and reusable protocol for sparse-text decision systems.

</details>


### [138] [A Rod Flow Model for Adam at the Edge of Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06821)
*Eric Regis,Sinho Chewi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文扩展了杆流（rod flow）方法，用于建模自适应梯度优化器（如Adam）在稳定性边界（edge of stability）附近的动力学行为。通过在参数和动量的联合相空间(w,m)中建模，并将二阶矩ν作为光滑辅助变量，作者为Adam、重型球动量、Nesterov动量以及RMSProp、Adam、NAdam的标量和逐分量版本共八种优化器开发了对应的杆流模型。实验表明，杆流相比传统的稳定流（stable flow）能更准确地追踪离散迭代在稳定性边界附近的轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作主要针对梯度下降（GD）在稳定性边界附近的连续时间建模，但缺乏对带动量或自适应方法的优化器（如Adam）的类似扩展。作者希望弥合这一空白，将杆流方法推广到更广泛的优化器族。

Method: 将连续的优化迭代视为一个扩展的一维对象（"杆"），在参数和动量联合相空间(w,m)中建立杆流微分方程，并将二阶矩ν作为光滑辅助变量处理。对Adam、重型球动量、Nesterov动量以及RMSProp、Adam、NAdam的标量和逐分量版本共八种优化器分别推导了对应的杆流模型。

Result: 在代表性机器学习架构上进行的实证评估显示，对于所有八种优化器，杆流在稳定性边界附近追踪离散迭代的准确性显著优于对应的稳定流（stable flow）。

Conclusion: 杆流方法可以成功地从梯度下降扩展到Adam等自适应动量优化器，为理解这些优化器在稳定性边界附近的动力学行为提供了更精确的连续时间建模工具。

Abstract: Cohen et al. (arXiv:2207.14484) observed that adaptive gradient methods such as Adam operate at the edge of stability. While there has been significant work on continuous-time modeling of gradient descent at the edge of stability, extending these models to momentum methods remains underdeveloped. In the gradient descent setting, Regis et al. (arXiv:2602.01480) introduced rod flow, which models consecutive iterates as an extended one-dimensional object -- a "rod." Here we extend rod flow to Adam by working in the joint phase space of parameters and first moment $(w, m)$ and treating the second moment $ν$ as a smooth auxiliary variable. We also develop rod flows for heavy ball momentum, Nesterov momentum, and scalar and per-component versions of RMSProp, Adam, and NAdam. For all eight optimizers, we empirically evaluate rod flow on representative machine learning architectures, where it tracks the discrete iterates through the edge-of-stability regime significantly more accurately than the corresponding stable flow.

</details>


### [139] [On Training in Imagination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06732)
*Nadav Timor,Ravid Shwartz-Ziv,Micah Goldblum,Yann LeCun,David Harel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于模型的强化学习中，在想象轨迹上训练策略时，学习到的动力学模型和奖励模型的误差如何影响回报和策略优化。首先，将误差分析扩展到含学习奖励模型的MDP，推导了最优样本分配比，并识别出低Lipschitz常数作为紧化误差界的需求。其次，分析了REINFORCE在噪声奖励下的梯度无偏性与方差特性，将预算分配问题简化为单变量优化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于模型的强化学习中，策略在由学习到的动力学模型和奖励模型生成的想象轨迹上训练，而不查询真实环境。这种范式下，模型误差对最终回报和策略优化的影响尚未被充分量化，特别是奖励模型误差的影响以及如何在有限预算下分配样本以获得更好性能。

Method: 1. 将Asadi et al. (2018)的误差分析从仅含学习动力学模型扩展到同时含学习奖励模型的MDP，推导最优样本分配比（动力学样本与奖励样本的比例）以最小化回报误差的界，假设幂律缩放。2. 识别出学习到的动力学、奖励和策略的低Lipschitz常数作为紧化误差界的关键需求，并将其与Wang et al. (2026)的时间拉直目标联系起来。3. 分析REINFORCE在零均值噪声奖励下的梯度无偏性和方差特性，将预算分配（更多低成本噪声轨迹 vs. 更少高成本精确轨迹）简化为单变量优化问题。

Result: 1. 推导了在含学习奖励模型的MDP中，最优样本分配比率的理论形式。2. 低Lipschitz常数被证明可以紧化回报误差界。3. 证明零均值奖励噪声使REINFORCE梯度估计无偏，且方差随轨迹数量增加而减小。4. 预算分配问题被简化为一个可解析求解的单变量优化问题，从而可刻画最优分配策略。

Conclusion: 本文为基于模型的强化学习中模型误差对策略优化的影响提供了定量分析框架，并给出了预算约束下样本分配的实用指导：低Lipschitz常数有利于减小回报误差，而噪声奖励虽然廉价但不会引入梯度偏差，在预算有限时可通过增加轨迹数量来抵消噪声方差，从而存在一个最优的样本分配策略。

Abstract: State-of-the-art model-based reinforcement learning methods train policies on imagined rollouts. These rollouts are trajectories generated by a learned dynamics model and are scored by a learned reward model, but without querying the true environment during policy updates. We study this training paradigm by quantifying how errors in learned dynamics and reward models affect returns and policy optimization. First, we extend the analysis of Asadi et al. (2018) to MDPs with learned reward models, and derive the optimal sample allocation--the ratio of dynamics samples to reward samples that minimizes a bound on return error under power-law scaling assumptions. We identify lower Lipschitz constants of the learned dynamics, reward, and policy as a representation desideratum that tightens this bound, and we connect this perspective to the temporal-straightening objective of Wang et al. (2026). Second, we examine how policy optimization with REINFORCE tolerates noisy rewards, which are often cheaper to obtain. We show that zero-mean reward noise leaves the gradient estimator unbiased and adds at most a variance term that decreases with the number of rollouts. This introduces a practical tradeoff: given a fixed budget, should one buy more rollouts with cheaper but noisier rewards, or fewer rollouts with more expensive but less noisy rewards? We reduce this choice to a one-dimensional optimization problem and characterize the optimum.

</details>


### [140] [When Descent Is Too Stable: Event-Triggered Hamiltonian Learning to Optimize](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06868)
*Yi Wang,Chandrajit Bajaj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SHAPE，一种基于自适应端口-哈密顿系统的非凸优化方法，通过将优化提升到增广相空间$(q, p)$，利用可学习哈密顿向量场实现结构化局部下降，并通过事件触发机制在检测到局部平衡时切换端口和记忆，从而在固定预算下平衡下降、利用与探索，避免陷入无信息局部极小值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定预算非凸优化中，优化器到达附近驻点后，剩余评估可能浪费在精细化不具信息量的局部最小值上；问题在于局部下降"过于稳定"而非不稳定。需要一种机制来决定何时下降、何时利用有前景的盆地、以及何时因停滞而触发移动到别处。

Method: SHAPE (Structured Adaptive Port-Hamiltonian Task-family Optimizer for Event-triggered Minima Hunting)：从梯度下降动力学出发，将优化提升到增广相空间$(q, p)$，其中$q$为候选解，$p$为余切变量携带方向敏感性，控制器$u$提供当前梯度信息。在每个阶段内，可学习的哈密顿向量场诱导结构化局部下降；跨阶段时，固定事件时钟在检测到局部平衡时更新端口和记忆。该设计保持无源性兼容结构，同一训练策略可处理干净、随机或估计的梯度输入。

Result: 在固定预算非凸优化任务上的实验表明，SHAPE相比固定策略优化器提升了"迄今最佳"性能。

Conclusion: 自适应哈密顿能量整形为在困难优化景观中平衡下降、探索和预算分配提供了有原则的机制。

Abstract: Fixed-budget nonconvex optimization can fail not because local descent is unstable, but because it is too stable: after reaching a nearby stationary point, an optimizer may spend the remaining evaluations refining an uninformative local minimum. We formulate this failure mode as a control problem over optimizer dynamics, where the learner must decide when to descend, when to exploit a promising basin, and when stagnation should trigger movement elsewhere. We introduce SHAPE, a structured adaptive port-Hamiltonian task-family optimizer for event-triggered minima hunting under local information. Starting from gradient-descent dynamics, SHAPE lifts optimization to an augmented phase space $(q, p)$, where the primal state $q$ represents the candidate solution, the cotangent variable $p$ carries directional sensitivity, and a controller $u$ provides processed information from current gradient oracle. Within each stage, a learned Hamiltonian vector field induces structured local descent; across stages, a fixed event clock in the implementation updates ports and memory when local equilibria are detected, with stage-dependent horizons treated in the analysis as a direct generalization. This design preserves a passivity-compatible structure while allowing the same trained policy to use clean, stochastic, or estimated gradient inputs. Experiments on fixed-budget nonconvex optimization tasks show that SHAPE improves best-so-far performance compared with fixed-policy optimizers. These results suggest that adaptive Hamiltonian energy shaping provides a principled mechanism for balancing descent, exploration, and budget allocation in difficult optimization landscapes.

</details>


### [141] [Gated QKAN-FWP: Scalable Quantum-inspired Sequence Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06734)
*Kuo-Chung Peng,Samuel Yen-Chi Chen,Jiun-Cheng Jiang,Chen-Yu Liu,En-Jui Kuo,Yun-Yuan Wang,Prayag Tiwari,Andrea Ceschini,Chi-Sheng Chen,Yu-Chao Hsu,Chun-Hua Lin,Tai-Yue Li,Antonello Rosato,Massimo Panella,Simon See,Saif Al-Kuwari,Kuan-Cheng Chen,Nan-Yow Chen,Hsi-Sheng Goan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出门控QKAN-FWP框架，融合快速权重编程与量子启发KAN，使用单量子比特数据重上传电路作为非线性激活，引入标量门控更新规则。在长时序列预测中，12.5k参数模型超越LSTM等经典基线（最高167k参数），并在真实量子处理器上验证了NISQ兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子快速权重编程（QFWP）依赖多量子比特架构，在NISQ设备上难以扩展且经典模拟成本高昂，亟需可扩展且NISQ兼容的量子启发序列建模方案。

Method: 1) 将快速权重编程与量子启发KAN结合，使用单量子比特数据重上传电路（DARUAN）作为可学习非线性激活函数；2) 引入标量门控快速权重更新规则，理论分析其自适应记忆核、几何有界性和可并行梯度路径。

Result: 在528步输入、132步预测的长时程太阳周期预测任务中，12.5k参数模型在缩放MSE、峰值幅度误差和峰值时序误差上均优于LSTM（25.9k-89.1k参数）、WaveNet-LSTM（167k）、RNN（11.5k）和ESN（132k）；在IonQ和IBM量子处理器上，1024次测量下相对MSE误差在0.1%以内。

Conclusion: 门控QKAN-FWP是一种可扩展、参数高效且NISQ兼容的量子启发序列建模方法。

Abstract: Fast Weight Programmers (FWPs) encode temporal dependencies through dynamically updated parameters rather than recurrent hidden states. Quantum FWPs (QFWPs) extend this idea with variational quantum circuits (VQCs), but existing implementations rely on multi-qubit architectures that are difficult to scale on noisy intermediate-scale quantum (NISQ) devices and expensive to simulate classically. We propose gated QKAN-FWP, a fast-weight framework that integrates FWP with Quantum-inspired Kolmogorov-Arnold Network (QKAN) using single-qubit data re-uploading circuits as learnable nonlinear activation, known as DatA Re-Uploading ActivatioN (DARUAN). We further introduce a scalar-gated fast-weight update rule that stabilizes parameter evolution, supported by a theoretical analysis of its adaptive memory kernel, geometric boundedness, and parallelizable gradient paths. We evaluate the framework across time-series benchmarks, MiniGrid reinforcement learning, and highlight real-world solar cycle forecasting as our main practical result. In the long-horizon setting with 528-month input window and 132-month forecast horizon, our 12.5k-parameter model achieves lower scaled Mean Square Error (MSE), peak amplitude error, and peak timing error than a suite of classical recurrent baselines with up to 13x more parameters, including Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks (25.9k-89.1k parameters), WaveNet-LSTM (167k), Vanilla recurrent neural network (11.5k), and a Modified Echo State Network (132k). To validate NISQ compatibility, we further deploy the trained fast programmer on IonQ and IBM Quantum processors, recovering forecasting accuracy within 0.1% relative MSE of the noiseless simulator at 1024 shots. These results position gated QKAN-FWP as a scalable, parameter-efficient, and NISQ-compatible approach to quantum-inspired sequence modeling.

</details>


### [142] [Almost Sure Convergence Rates of Stochastic Approximation and Reinforcement Learning via a Poisson-Moreau Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07104)
*Xinyu Liu,Zixuan Xie,Shangtong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为一类期望更新具有压缩性的随机逼近算法（涵盖Q学习、线性时序差分学习等强化学习算法）建立了在马尔可夫噪声下的几乎必然收敛速率。对幂律学习率（指数η∈(1/2,1)）得到接近o(n^{1-2η})的速率；对调和学习率O(n^{-1})得到接近o(n^{-1})的速率，接近i.i.d.噪声下的最优速率。关键创新是基于泊松方程校正马尔可夫噪声的Lyapunov漂移构造，结合Moreau-envelope光滑化技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在马尔可夫噪声下建立随机逼近和强化学习算法的几乎必然收敛速率是一个基础性的理论挑战，现有结果尚不完善。

Method: 提出一种新型Lyapunov漂移构造，通过对压缩映射应用基于泊松方程的校正项来处理马尔可夫噪声，并结合Moreau-envelope光滑化技术。

Result: 对于幂律学习率O(n^{-η})（η∈(1/2,1)），获得任意接近o(n^{1-2η})的几乎必然收敛速率；对于调和学习率O(n^{-1})，获得任意接近o(n^{-1})的速率，接近迭代对数定律给出的最优速率O(n^{-1}log log n)。

Conclusion: 首次为马尔可夫噪声下的压缩类随机逼近算法建立了接近最优的几乎必然收敛速率，显著推进了该领域的理论进展。

Abstract: Establishing almost sure convergence rates for stochastic approximation and reinforcement learning under Markovian noise is a fundamental theoretical challenge. We make progress towards this challenge for a class of stochastic approximation algorithms whose expected updates are contractive, a setting that arises in many reinforcement learning algorithms such as $Q$-learning and linear temporal difference learning. Specifically, for a power-law learning rate $O(n^{-η})$ with $η\in (1/2, 1)$, we obtain an almost sure convergence rate arbitrarily close to $o(n^{1 - 2η})$. For a harmonic learning rate $O(n^{-1})$, we obtain an almost sure convergence rate arbitrarily close to $o(n^{-1})$, which we argue is a strong result because it is close to the optimal rate $O(n^{-1}\log\log n)$ given by the law of the iterated logarithm (for a special case of i.i.d. noise). Key to our analysis is a novel Lyapunov drift construction that applies a Poisson-equation based correction for Markovian noise to the well-established Moreau-envelope smoothing for the contractive mapping.

</details>


### [143] [Geometric Kolmogorov--Arnold Network (GeoKAN)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06740)
*Abhijit Sen,Bikram Keshari Parida,Giridas Maiti,Mahima Arya,Denys I. Bondar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GeoKAN是一类几何感知的KAN模型，通过学习对角黎曼度量实现输入空间的几何变形，从而在自适应坐标下进行函数逼近，适用于包含剧烈变化、刚性和局部化特征的科学机器学习与微分方程问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的KAN模型在固定的欧几里得输入坐标下进行逼近，缺乏对数据几何结构的自适应能力，难以高效处理具有剧烈变化、局部化特征或强非均匀性的科学计算问题。

Method: GeoKAN通过学习一个对角黎曼度量来扭曲输入空间，在基展开和特征混合之前对输入进行几何自适应变换。该度量通过局部长度缩放和体积畸变提供几何归纳偏置。论文提出了三种主要变体：GeoKAN-NNMetric、GeoKAN-γ和LM-KAN，其中LM-KAN又包含RBF、Wav和Fourier三种基函数版本。

Result: GeoKAN通过拉伸变化剧烈区域并压缩平滑区域，以任务相关的方式重新分配表示分辨率，使模型将计算容量集中在最需要的地方，从而在尖峰、刚性、局部化和强非均匀性场景下表现出色。

Conclusion: GeoKAN作为一种几何感知的KAN模型，在科学机器学习和微分方程问题中具有良好的适用性，能够有效处理传统方法难以应对的非均匀和局部化挑战。

Abstract: We introduce Geometric Kolmogorov--Arnold Networks (GeoKANs), a family of geometry-aware KAN-type models in which approximation is carried out in learned, geometry-adapted coordinates rather than in fixed Euclidean input coordinates. GeoKAN achieves this by learning a diagonal Riemannian metric that warps the input before basis expansion and feature mixing. The learned metric provides a geometric inductive bias through local length scaling and volume distortion, and in physics-informed settings it also affects the differential structure seen by the model. Within this framework, we develop three main variants, namely GeoKAN-NNMetric, GeoKAN-$γ$, and LM-KAN. For LM-KAN, we further consider three basis-specific versions, LM-KAN-RBF, LM-KAN-Wav, and LM-KAN-Fourier. These variants allow us to study geometry-aware KAN models both as general function approximators and as surrogates in physics-informed learning. By stretching regions with rapid variation and compressing smoother regions, GeoKAN reallocates representational resolution in a task-dependent manner, allowing the model to place capacity where it is most needed. As a result, GeoKAN is well suited to sharp, stiff, localized, and strongly non-uniform regimes arising in scientific machine learning and differential-equation problems.

</details>


### [144] [A Closed-Form Upper Bound for Admissible Learning-Rate Steps in Belief-Space Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06741)
*Zixi Li,Youzhen Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出学习率步长不应被视为超参数，而应通过局部置信空间计算得出：在概率单纯形上的投影前向步模型中，可容许步长的上界可通过KL/Bregman几何中的收缩性公式精确计算，而非经验调优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上将学习率步长视为需要人工调优的超参数，缺乏理论指导。作者希望从几何视角（KL/Bregman散度）建立步长上界的解析公式，消除经验调优的模糊性。

Method: 将参数更新建模为概率单纯形上的投影前向步，利用Bregman散度（KL散度）的几何性质，将可容许性（admissibility）等价于该几何中的收缩性（contractivity），从而推导出步长上界的闭合表达式。

Result: 发现可容许步长的上界不是通过试错确定的超参数，而是由KL/Bregman几何结构直接决定的公式化结果。

Conclusion: 学习率步长应基于局部置信空间的几何分析计算，而非依赖启发式调优，这为自适应步长策略提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Learning-rate steps are usually treated as hyperparameters. This paper isolates a local beliefspace calculation: when an update is modeled as a projected forward step on the probability simplex, admissibility means contractivity in the natural KL/Bregman geometry. Under this model, the upper bound of an admissible step is not a tuning slogan but a formula.

</details>


### [145] [Gradient Extrapolation-Based Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06755)
*Ismam Nur Swapnil,Aranya Saha,Tanvir Ahmed Khan,Mohammad Ariful Haque,Ser-Nam Lim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GXPO提出一种梯度外推策略优化方法，仅用三次反向传播近似多步前瞻，作为GRPO的即插即用替代方案，在数学推理任务上提升了1.65~5.00个点的pass@1，并实现了最高4倍的步数加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准GRPO-style训练只使用当前步更新，而完整多步前瞻虽能提供更优更新方向但计算成本过高（需大量反向传播），因此希望在近似多步前瞻效果的同时保持计算高效。

Method: GXPO在活跃阶段仅用三次反向传播：两次快速优化步测量梯度变化，预测虚拟K步前瞻点，将策略部分移向该点，再用新位置的真实梯度进行修正更新。当外推信号不稳定时自动回退到标准GRPO。

Result: 在Qwen2.5和Llama的数学推理实验中，GXPO相比GRPO提升pass@1达+1.65~+5.00点，相比最强SFPO提升+0.14~+1.28点；达到GRPO峰值精度时实现最高4倍步数加速、2.33倍实际时间加速和1.33倍反向传播加速。

Conclusion: GXPO通过梯度外推以极低开销实现近似多步前瞻，在保持GRPO兼容性的同时显著提升推理性能和训练效率。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning is widely used to improve the reasoning ability of large language models, especially when answers can be automatically checked. Standard GRPO-style training updates the model using only the current step, while full multi-step lookahead can give a better update direction but is too expensive because it needs many backward passes. We propose Gradient Extrapolation-Based Policy Optimization (GXPO), a plug-compatible policy-update rule for GRPO-style reasoning RL. GXPO approximates a longer local lookahead using only three backward passes during an active phase. It reuses the same batch of rollouts, rewards, advantages, and GRPO loss, so it does not require new rollouts or reward computation at the lookahead points. GXPO takes two fast optimizer steps, measures how the gradients change, predicts a virtual K-step lookahead point, moves the policy partway toward that point, and then applies a corrective update using the true gradient at the new position. When the lookahead signal becomes unstable, GXPO automatically switches back to standard single-pass GRPO. We also give a plain-gradient-descent surrogate analysis that explains when the extrapolation is exact and where its local errors come from. Across Qwen2.5 and Llama math-reasoning experiments, GXPO improves the average sampled pass@1 by +1.65 to +5.00 points over GRPO and by +0.14 to +1.28 points over the strongest SFPO setting, while keeping the active-phase cost fixed at three backward passes. It also achieves up to 4.00x step speedup, 2.33x wall-clock speedup, and 1.33x backward-pass speedup in reaching GRPO's peak accuracy.

</details>


### [146] [Sample Complexity of Stochastic Optimization with Integer Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07239)
*Hongyu Cheng,Yinghao Zheng,Marco Molinaro,Amitabh Basu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了整数随机优化的样本复杂度，发现相较于连续优化，整数优化在某些情况下需要更多样本，在某些情况下需要更少样本，具体取决于目标函数和约束的结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解整数随机优化相较于对应连续优化问题的统计复杂度差异，揭示目标与约束结构对样本复杂度的影响。

Method: 通过理论分析，分别针对三类设置（Lipschitz目标+ℓ∞球约束、Lipschitz目标+ℓ₂球约束、强凸光滑目标）推导并对比整数优化与连续优化的样本复杂度下界与上界。

Result: 1. ℓ∞球上Lipschitz目标：混合整数非线性非凸优化的统计复杂度与带界约束的随机线性优化完全相同。2. ℓ₂球上Lipschitz目标：整数优化在特定区间内样本复杂度严格小于连续优化。3. 强凸光滑目标：整数优化需要Ω(1/ε²)样本（连续优化仅需O(1/ε)），复杂度更高。

Conclusion: 整数优化的样本复杂度与连续优化存在本质差异，不可简单等同；结构（范数类型、凸性）显著影响复杂度对比关系。

Abstract: We establish sample complexity results for stochastic optimization over the integers, especially with a view to understand the complexity with respect to the corresponding continuous optimization problem. We show that integer optimization can sometimes require strictly more samples and sometimes strictly smaller number of samples, depending on the structure of the objective and constraints. 1. For Lipschitz objectives over subsets of the $\ell_\infty$ ball, the statistical complexity of general stochastic mixed-integer, nonlinear, nonconvex optimization is exactly the same as stochastic linear optimization with just bound constraints. 2. For Lipschitz objectives over subsets of the $\ell_2$ ball, we show that integer optimization can require strictly *smaller* sample size compared to the continuous setting in a certain regime. To get to this result, we also establish tight sample complexity results for nonconvex continuous stochastic optimization which, to the best of our knowledge, do not appear in prior work. 3. For strongly convex, smooth objectives, integer optimization has high statistical complexity compared to the continuous setting. In particular, we show that integer optimization requires $Ω(1/ε^2)$ samples to report an $ε$-approximate solution, compared to the well-known $O(1/ε)$ sample complexity from the continuous optimization literature.

</details>


### [147] [Convex Optimization with Nested Evolving Feasible Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07386)
*Karthick Krishna M.,Haricharan Balasundaram,Rahul Vaze*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了凸优化中可行集随时间嵌套演化的问题（CONES），提出了两种算法：Lazy算法在凸损失函数下实现了O(T^{1-β})后悔度与O(T^β)移动成本的权衡；Frugal算法在强凸或α-sharp损失函数下同时达到零后悔度和O(log T)移动成本，并证明了该移动成本下界为Ω(log T)，从而验证了Frugal的最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典的嵌套凸集追逐问题推广到优化导向的框架（CONES），目标是在可行集随时间嵌套演化的动态环境下，同时最小化在线算法相对于 hindsight 静态最优基准的后悔度与总移动成本，并保证所有时间步的可行性。

Method: 针对凸损失函数提出Lazy算法，通过调节参数β实现后悔度与移动成本的权衡；针对强凸或α-sharp损失函数提出Frugal算法，利用问题的强凸性/锐利性结构实现零后悔与对数级移动成本；并通过下界分析证明Frugal的最优性。

Result: Lazy算法在凸损失下达到O(T^{1-β})后悔度和O(T^β)移动成本；Frugal算法在强凸或α-sharp损失下达到零后悔度和O(log T)移动成本；且任何具有o(T)后悔度的在线算法移动成本至少为Ω(log T)。

Conclusion: Frugal算法在强凸和α-sharp场景下是最优的，其移动成本下界匹配上界；CONES框架统一并推广了嵌套凸集追逐问题，为动态可行集下的在线优化提供了理论保证。

Abstract: Convex Optimization with Nested Evolving Feasible Sets (CONES)} is considered where the objective function $f$ remains fixed but the feasible region evolves over time as a nested sequence $S_1 \supseteq S_2 \supseteq \cdots \supseteq S_T$. The goal of an online algorithm is to simultaneously minimize the regret with respect to hindsight static optimal benchmark and the total movement cost while ensuring feasibility at all times. CONES is an optimization-oriented generalization of the well-known nested convex body chasing problem. When the loss function is convex, we propose a lazy-algorithm and show that it achieves $O(T^{1-β}), O(T^β)$ simultaneous regret and movement cost for any $β\in (0,1]$, over a time horizon of $T$.
